<p><span class="pn">17</span> The No Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>) sign, the No U-Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>) sign, and the combination No U or Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>) sign shall not be used at approaches to roundabouts to prohibit drivers from turning left onto the circulatory roadway of a roundabout.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> At roundabouts, the use of <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a> signs to prohibit left turns onto the circulatory roadway might confuse drivers about the possible legal turning movements around the roundabout. Roundabout Circulation (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-5p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-5P.svg">R6-5P</a>) plaques (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b51">Section 2B.51</a>) and/or ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-2.svg">R6-2</a>) signs are appropriate to indicate the travel direction within a roundabout.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b27" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.27 Intersection Lane Control Signs (R3-5 through R3-8)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.27 &middot; manual page 183 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.27/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Intersection Lane Control signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>), if used, shall require road users in certain lanes to turn, shall permit turns from a lane where such turns would otherwise not be permitted, shall require a road user to stay in the same lane and proceed straight through an intersection, or shall indicate permitted movements from a lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Intersection Lane Control signs have three applications: A.Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a> series and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> series) signs, B.Optional Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-6</a> series) signs, and C.Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When Intersection Lane Control signs are mounted overhead, each sign used should be placed over the lane or a projection of the lane to which it applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> On signalized approaches where through lanes that become mandatory turn lanes, multiple-lane turns that include shared lanes for through and turning movements, or other lane-use regulations are present that would be unexpected by unfamiliar road users, overhead Intersection Lane Control signs should be installed at the signalized location over the appropriate lanes or projections thereof and in advance of the intersection over the appropriate lanes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Where overhead mounting on the approach is impracticable for the Advance and/or Intersection lane Control signs, one of the following alternatives should be employed: A.At locations where through lanes become mandatory turn lanes, a Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) sign should be post-mounted on the left-hand side of the roadway where a through lane is becoming a mandatory left-turn lane on a one-way street or where a median of sufficient width for the signs is available, or on the right-hand side of the roadway where a through lane is becoming a mandatory right-turn lane. B.At locations where a through lane is becoming a mandatory left-turn lane on a two-way street where a median of sufficient width for the signs is not available, and at locations where multiple-lane turns that include shared lanes for through and turning movements are present, an Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) sign should be post-mounted in a prominent location in advance of the intersection, and consideration should be given to the use of an oversized version in accordance with <a href="#fig-2b-1">Table 2B-1</a><span class="ca">and 2B-1(CA)</span>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for error in Paragraph 5 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Use of an overhead sign for one approach lane should not require installation of overhead signs for the other lanes of that approach.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Intersection Lane Control signs may be omitted where: A.A turn bay has been provided by physical construction or pavement markings, and B.Only the road users using such turn bays are permitted to make a turn in that direction.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> At roundabouts, Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-6</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) signs may display any of the arrow symbol options shown in <a href="#fig-2b-5">Figure 2B-5</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r61ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r61ca.webp">R61(CA)</a> Series and R73(CA) Series) signs (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-4ca">Figure 2B-4(CA)</a>) may be used to indicate  the types of movements permitted at intersections.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b26">Section 2B.26</a> for use of the R73(CA) series signs when a NO U-TURN is required.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b28" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.28 Mandatory Movement Lane Control Signs (R3-5, R3-5a, R3-7, R3-19 Series, and R3-20) and Plaques

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.28 &middot; manual page 184 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.28/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5a.svg">R3-5a</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>), if used, shall indicate only the single vehicle movement that is required from the lane.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5a.svg">R3-5a</a>) symbol signs shall include the legend ONLY and shall be mounted overhead over the specific lanes <span class="ca">or projections thereof</span> to which they apply (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b27">Section 2B.27</a>). The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign shall be for post-mounting only. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign shall not be mounted at the far side of the intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When the mandatory movement applies to lanes exclusively designated for HOV traffic, the HOV 2+ (R3- 5cP) supplemental plaque shall be used. When the mandatory movement applies to lanes that are not HOV facilities, but are lanes exclusively designated for buses and/or taxis, the TAXI LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5dP.svg">R3-5dP</a>) and/or BUS LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5gp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5gP.svg">R3-5gP</a>) supplemental plaques shall be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) sign shall be located in advance of the intersection, such as near the upstream end of the mandatory movement lane, and/or at the near side of the intersection where the regulation applies.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The use of the Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a>) word message sign should be limited to only locations that are adjacent to the full-width portion of a mandatory turn lane. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign should not be installed adjacent to a through lane in advance of a turn bay taper or adjacent to a turn bay taper.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs should be accompanied by lane-use arrow markings, especially where traffic volumes are high, where there is a high percentage of commercial vehicles, or where other distractions exist.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Where the restriction does not apply to buses or bicycles an EXCEPT BUSES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7aP.svg">R3-7aP</a>) or EXCEPT BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7bP.svg">R3-7bP</a>) plaque should be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The Through Only (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5a.svg">R3-5a</a>) sign may be used to require a road user in a particular lane to proceed straight through an intersection.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The diamond symbol may be used instead of the word message HOV on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5cP.svg">R3-5cP</a> supplemental plaque.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Where a mandatory left or U-turn lane is added at a median location, a LANE FOR LEFT TURN ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19.svg">R3-19</a>) or LANE FOR U TURN ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19a.svg">R3-19a</a>) sign may be post-mounted on the median at the beginning of the taper. Where a U turn and a left turn are both allowed, a LANE FOR U AND LEFT TURNS ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19b.svg">R3-19b</a>) sign may be used. Where a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19.svg">R3-19</a> series sign is used, Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs along the turn lane in the median may be omitted.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-19/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-19.svg">R3-19</a> series signs may be used where the added median turn lane is separated from the through lanes by a channelizing or divisional island.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> On an approach to a mandatory turn lane where traffic regularly enters the shoulder to access the turn lane inappropriately, creating safety or operational issues, a DO NOT DRIVE ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-17/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-17.svg">R4-17</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b43">Section 2B.43</a>) may be used to supplement the standard Mandatory Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5.svg">R3-5</a> and/or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> series) signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> Refer to CVC 22101 for Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Signs or markings should be repeated in advance of mandatory turn lanes when necessary to prevent entrapment and to help  motorists select the appropriate lane before reaching the end of the line of waiting vehicles.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c50">Section 2C.50</a> for warning signs used in advance of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-7</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) LANE FREEWAY ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r18bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r18bca.webp">R18B(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-4ca">Figure 2B-4(CA)</a>) should be used on non-freeway  facilities to indicate that a particular lane only leads to a freeway entrance and on to the freeway. The sign should be used in  conjunction with, and at the beginning of, the 8 inch solid white lines indicating that traffic in that lane has a mandatory movement  leading to a freeway</p>
</div>

<span id="2b29" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.29 Optional Movement Lane Control Signs (R3-6 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.29 &middot; manual page 185 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.29/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Optional Movement Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-6</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6a.svg">R3-6a</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-6b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-6b.svg">R3-6b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>), if used, shall be used for two or more movements from a specific lane or to emphasize permitted movements. The Optional Movement Lane Control sign shall be mounted overhead over the specific lane <span class="ca">or projections thereof</span> to which it applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the Optional Movement Lane Control signs shall indicate all permissible movements from specific lanes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Because more than one movement is permitted from the lane, the word message ONLY shall not be used on an Optional Movement Lane Control sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Optional Movement Lane Control signs shall be used for two or more movements from a specific lane where a movement, not allowed by State statute or local ordinance, is permitted.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The Optional Movement Lane Control signs shall not be used alone to effect a turn prohibition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If used, the Optional Movement Lane Control sign should be located overhead in advance of the intersection, such as near the upstream end of an adjacent mandatory movement lane, and/or overhead at the intersection where the regulation applies.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b30" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.30 Advance Intersection Lane Control Signs (R3-8 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.30 &middot; manual page 186 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.30/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8a.svg">R3-8a</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8b.svg">R3-8b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may <span class="ca">should</span>be used to indicate the configuration of all lanes ahead <span class="ca">where there are optional lanes, mandatory turn lanes without turning bays or un-shadowed  turn lanes</span>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The word messages ONLY, THRU, HOV 2+, TAXI, <span class="ca">FWY,</span><span class="ca">TRUCKS OK,</span> BUS, or BIKE, or the bicycle symbol, may be used within the border in combination with the arrow symbols of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign series. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5cP.svg">R3-5cP</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5dP.svg">R3-5dP</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5gp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5gP.svg">R3-5gP</a> supplemental plaques may be installed at the top outside border of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign over the applicable lane designation on the sign. The diamond symbol may be used instead of the word message HOV. The minimum allowable vehicle occupancy requirement may vary based on the level established for a particular facility.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Where a bicycle lane is between two general-purpose lanes the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series signs may be modified to show the bicycle lane with a white legend on a black background in accordance with designs of the R3-8x series signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> When used, an Advance Intersection Lane Control sign should be placed at an adequate distance in advance of the intersection, either along the lane tapers or at the beginning of the turn lane so that road users can select the appropriate lane (see <a href="#fig-2a-4">Figure 2A-4</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04a</span> <span class="ca">Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8a.svg">R3-8a</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8b.svg">R3-8b</a>) signs may be installed at the intersection</span><span class="ca">of short storage capacity.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> An Advance Intersection Lane Control sign may be repeated closer to the intersection along the approach for additional emphasis.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> An Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) sign shall not be mounted at the far side of an intersection to which it applies.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Where three or more approach lanes are available to traffic, Advance Intersection Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> series) signs, if used, shall be post-mounted in advance of the intersection and shall not be mounted overhead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> When only the two outermost lanes of the roadway are shown on a <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-5bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-5bP.svg">R3-5bP</a> or R3- 5fP plaque shall be mounted above the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-8.svg">R3-8</a> sign.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b31" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.31 RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT Signs (R3-33, R3-33a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.31 &middot; manual page 186 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.31/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used to supplement an overhead EXIT ONLY guide sign to inform road users that traffic in the right-hand (left-hand) lane of a roadway that is approaching a grade-separated interchange is required to depart the roadway on the exit ramp at the next interchange.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The RIGHT (LEFT) LANE MUST EXIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) shall be used to indicate a freeway lane drop.  The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a> sign shall be placed at the beginning of the 8 inch solid white line approaching the exit ramp.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33a__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33a</a> sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used in place of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a> sign where the roadside width is limited and will not accommodate the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a> sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02a</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r18cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r18cca.webp">R18C(CA)</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r18dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r18dca.webp">R18D(CA)</a> sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-4ca">Figure 2B-4(CA)</a>) may be used in place of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-33a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-33a__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-33a</a> sign where there are  multiple lanes required to depart the roadway on the exit ramp at the next interchange.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c50">Section 2C.50</a> contains information regarding a warning sign that can be used in advance of lane drops at grade- separated interchanges.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b32" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.32 Two-Way Left-Turn-Only Signs (R3-9a and R3-9b) and Plaques

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.32 &middot; manual page 187 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.32/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A Two-Way Left-Turn-Only (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9a.svg">R3-9a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9b.svg">R3-9b</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) should be used in conjunction with the required pavement markings where a non-reversible lane is reserved for the exclusive use of left- turning vehicles in either direction and is not used for passing, overtaking, or through travel.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-6"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0237.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0237.webp" alt="Figure 2B-6. Center and Reversible Lane Control Signs and Plaques R3-9a R3-9b R3-9e R3-9f R3-9i OR R3-9h R3-9cP R3-9dP OR R3-9g"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-6. Center and Reversible Lane Control Signs and Plaques R3-9a R3-9b R3-9e R3-9f R3-9i OR R3-9h R3-9cP R3-9dP OR R3-9g <span class="src-page">p 237</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The post-mounted <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9b.svg">R3-9b</a> sign may be used as an alternate to or a supplement to the overhead <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9a.svg">R3-9a</a> sign. The legend BEGIN or END may be used within the border of the main sign itself, or on an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9cP.svg">R3-9cP</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9dP.svg">R3-9dP</a> plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) mounted immediately above it.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Signing is especially helpful to drivers in areas where the two-way left-turn-only maneuver is new, in areas subject to environmental conditions that frequently obscure the pavement markings, and on peripheral streets with two-way left-turn-only lanes leading to an extensive system of routes with two-way left-turn-only lanes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Refer to <a href="#fig-3a-108ca">Figure 3A-108(CA)</a> and <a href="#fig-3b-7ca">Figure 3B-7(CA)</a> for pavement marking applications for Two-Way Left Turn Lanes.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b33" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.33 BEGIN and END Plaques (R3-9cP and R3-9dP)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.33 &middot; manual page 187 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.33/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The BEGIN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9cp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9cP.svg">R3-9cP</a>) or END (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9dp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9dP.svg">R3-9dP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>), mounted directly above a regulatory sign, may be used to inform road users of the location where a regulatory condition begins or ends.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b34" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.34 Reversible Lane Control Signs (R3-9e through R3-9i)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.34 &middot; manual page 187 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.34/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A reversible lane may be used for through traffic (with left turns either permitted or prohibited) in alternating directions during different periods of the day, and the lane may be used for exclusive left turns in one or both directions during other periods of the day as well. Reversible Lane Control (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9i/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9i.svg">R3-9i</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) may be either static type or changeable message type. These signs may be either post- mounted or overhead. Lane-use control signals (see Chapter 4T) may also be used for reversible lanes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Where it is determined by an engineering study that lane-use control signals or physical barriers are not necessary, the lane shall be controlled by overhead Reversible Lane Control signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-7">Figure 2B-7</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Post-mounted Reversible Lane Control signs shall be used only as a supplement to overhead signs or signals. Post-mounted signs shall be identical in design to the overhead signs and an additional legend such as CENTER LANE shall be added to the top of the sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9f/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9f.svg">R3-9f</a>) to indicate which lane is controlled.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Reversing traffic flow may be controlled with pavement markings and Reversible Lane Control signs (without the use of lane-use control signals), when all of the following conditions are met: A.Only one lane is being reversed, B.An engineering study indicates that the use of Reversible Lane Control signs alone would result in an acceptable level of safety and efficiency, and C.There are no unusual or complex operations in the reversible lane pattern.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Reversible Lane Control signs shall contain the legend or symbols designating the allowable uses of the lane and the time periods such uses are allowed. Where symbols and legends are used, their meanings shall be as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-2">Table 2B-2</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Reversible Lane Control signs shall consist of a white background with a black legend and border, except for the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> sign, where the color red is used for the X symbol.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Symbol signs, such as the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> sign, shall consist of the appropriate symbol in the upper portion of the sign with the appropriate times of the day and days of the week below it. All times of the day and days of the week shall be accounted for on the sign to eliminate confusion to the road user.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> In situations where more than one message is conveyed to the road user, such as on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9e/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9e.svg">R3-9e</a> sign, the sign legend shall be arranged as follows: A.The prohibition or restriction message is the primary legend and shall be on the top for word message signs and to the far left for symbol signs, B.The permissive use message shall be displayed as the second legend, and C.The OTHER TIMES message shall be displayed at the bottom for word message signs and to the far right for symbol signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The symbol signs may also include a downward-pointing arrow with the legend THIS LANE. The term OTHER TIMES may be used for either the symbol or word message sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> A Reversible Lane Control sign shall be mounted over the approximate center of the lane that is being reversed.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> If the vertical or horizontal alignment is curved to the degree that a driver would be unable to see at least one sign, and preferably two signs, then additional overhead signs shall be installed. The placement of the signs shall be such that the driver will have a definite indication of the lanes specifically reserved for use at any given time. Special consideration shall be given to major generators introducing traffic between the normal sign placement.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Transitions at the entry to and exit from a section of roadway with reversible lanes shall include advance signs to notify or warn drivers of the boundaries of the reversible lane controls. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9g/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9g__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9g</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9h__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9h</a> signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) shall be used for this purpose.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> More than one End Reverse Lane (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9i/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9i.svg">R3-9i</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-6">Figure 2B-6</a>) may be used at the termination of the reversible lane to emphasize the importance of the message.</p>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Where longitudinal barriers separate opposing directions of traffic, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9g/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9g__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9g</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-9h/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-9h__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R3-9h</a> signs may be omitted.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> Flashing beacons, if used to supplement the overhead Reversible Lane Control signs, shall comply with the applicable requirements for flashing beacons in Chapter4S.</p>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> When used in conjunction with Reversible Lane Control signs, the Turn Prohibition (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1.svg">R3-1</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>) signs shall be mounted overhead and separate from the Reversible Lane Control signs. The Turn Prohibition signs shall be designed and installed in accordance with <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b26">Section 2B.26</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> For additional emphasis, a supplemental plaque stating the distance of the prohibition, such as NEXT 1 MILE, should be added to the Turn Prohibition signs that are used in conjunction with Reversible Lane Control signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> If used, overhead signs should be located at intervals not greater than ¼ mile. The bottom of the overhead Reversible Lane Control signs should not be more than 19 feet above the pavement grade.</p>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> Where more than one sign is used at the termination of a reversible lane, they should be at least 250 feet apart. Longer distances between signs are appropriate for streets with speeds over 35 mph, but the separation should not exceed 1,000 feet.</p>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> Because left-turning vehicles have a significant impact on the safety and efficiency of a reversible lane operation, if a mandatory left-turn lane or two-way left-turn lane cannot be incorporated into the lane- use pattern for a particular peak or off-peak period, consideration should be given to prohibiting left turns and U-turns during that time period.</p>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> Reversible Lane Control signs and parking signs should be consistent in message during the same operational periods.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b35" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.35 Jughandle Signs (R3-23, R3-24, R3-25, and R3-26 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.35 &middot; manual page 188 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.35/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A jughandle turn is a left turn or U-turn that because of special geometry is made by initially making a right turn. This type of turn can increase the operational efficiency of a roadway by eliminating the need for mandatory left-turn lanes and can increase the operational efficiency of a traffic control signal by eliminating the need for protected left-turn phases. A jughandle turn can also provide an opportunity for trucks and commercial vehicles to make a U-turn where the median and roadway are not of sufficient width to accommodate a traditional U-turn by these vehicles.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> <a href="#fig-2b-8">Figure 2B-8</a> shows the various signs that can be used for signing jughandle turns. <a href="#fig-2b-9">Figure 2B-9</a> shows examples of regulatory and destination guide signing for various types of jughandle turns.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline multi"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-8"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0239.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0239.webp" alt="Figure 2B-8. Jughandle Regulatory Signs R3-23a R3-23 R3-24 R3-24a R3-24b R3-25 R3-25a R3-25b R3-26 R3-26a"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-8. Jughandle Regulatory Signs R3-23a R3-23 R3-24 R3-24a R3-24b R3-25 R3-25a R3-25b R3-26 R3-26a <span class="src-page">p 239</span></figcaption></figure><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-9"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0240.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0240.webp" alt="Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-9. Examples of Applications of Jughandle Regulatory and Guide Signing <span class="src-page">p 240</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> On multi-lane roadways, since road users generally anticipate that they need to be in the left- hand lane when approaching a location where they desire to turn left or make a U-turn, an ALL TURNS FROM RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-23/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-23.svg">R3-23</a>) or a U TURN FROM RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-23a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-23a.svg">R3-23a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-8">Figure 2B-8</a>) shall be installed in advance of the location to inform drivers that left turns and/or U-turns will be made from the right-hand lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Where a median of sufficient width is available, supplemental regulatory or guide signs may also be placed on the left-hand side of the roadway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-24/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-24.svg">R3-24</a> series sign with an upward diagonal arrow pointing to the right if the jughandle entrance is designed as an exit ramp (see Drawings A and B in <a href="#fig-2b-9">Figure 2B-9</a>), or the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-25.svg">R3-25</a> series sign with a horizontal arrow pointing to the right if the jughandle entrance is designed as an intersection, shall be installed on the right-hand side of the roadway at the entrance to the jughandle. The legend on the sign shall be ALL TURNS, U TURN, or U AND LEFT TURNS, as appropriate.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If the jughandle is designed such that the jughandle entrance is downstream of the location where the turn would normally have been made (see Drawing C in <a href="#fig-2b-9">Figure 2B-9</a>), the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-26.svg">R3-26</a> series sign with an arrow pointing straight upward shall be installed on the right-hand side of the roadway at the intersection to inform road users that they need to proceed straight through the intersection in order to make a left turn or U-turn. The legend on the sign shall be U TURN or U AND LEFT TURNS, as appropriate.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-24/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-24.svg">R3-24</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-25.svg">R3-25</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-26.svg">R3-26</a> series of signs are designed to be mounted below conventional guide signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c12">Section 2C.12</a> contains information regarding the use of advisory exit and ramp speed signs for exit ramps.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2d/#2d40">Section 2D.40</a> contains information regarding the use of guide signs for jughandles.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Passing, Keep Right, And Slow Traffic Signs</div>


<span id="2b36" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.36 DO NOT PASS Sign (R4-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.36 &middot; manual page 190 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.36/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The DO NOT PASS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-1.svg">R4-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used in addition to pavement markings (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b03">Section 3B.03</a>) to emphasize the restriction on passing.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">When used,</span>the Do Not Pass sign may<span class="ca">shall</span>be used at the beginning of, and at intervals within, a zone through which sight distance is restricted or where other conditions make overtaking and passing inappropriate.</p>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-1.svg">R4-1</a> sign shall not be used in the freeway or express way, etc., where two or more lanes remain after a lane  dropped or was reduced. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b36">Section 2B.36</a>.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If signing is needed on the left-hand side of the roadway for additional emphasis, NO PASSING ZONE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w14-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W14-3.svg">W14-3</a>) signs may be used (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c53">Section 2C.53</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Standards for determining the location and extent of no-passing zone pavement markings are set forth in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b03">Section 3B.03</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Typical examples of where the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-1.svg">R4-1</a> sign could be applied are shown in Figures 3B-14(CA) and 3B-104(CA).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-1.svg">R4-1</a> sign may be used in conjunction with temporary traffic control signs.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b37" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.37 PASS WITH CARE Sign (R4-2)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.37 &middot; manual page 190 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.37/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The PASS WITH CARE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-2.svg">R4-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) should be installed at the downstream end of a no-passing zone if a DO NOT PASS sign has been installed at the upstream end of the zone.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b38" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.38 KEEP RIGHT EXCEPT TO PASS Sign (R4-16), SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT Sign (R4-3), and TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE Sign (R4-5)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.38 &middot; manual page 190 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.38/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The KEEP RIGHT EXCEPT TO PASS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-16.svg">R4-16</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used on roadways where there are two lanes in one direction of travel to direct drivers to stay in the right-hand lane except when they are passing another vehicle. <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 21659.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the KEEP RIGHT EXCEPT TO PASS sign should be installed at or just beyond the beginning of a two-lane section of roadway and at selected locations along two-lane roadways where additional emphasis is needed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-3.svg">R4-3</a>) or the TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-5.svg">R4-5</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used on multi-lane through roadways to improve capacity or reduce unnecessary lane changing due to the presence of slower vehicles that impede the normal flow of traffic.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="ca">The SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-3.svg">R4-3</a>) sign shall be used at the beginning of passing lanes. Refer to CVC 21654. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT sign or the TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE sign should be installed at or just beyond the beginning of a multi-lane roadway section or at the beginning of an extra lane provided for trucks and/or other slow-moving traffic, and at selected locations where there is a tendency on the part of some road users to drive in the left-hand lane(or lanes) below the normal speed of traffic. These signs should not be used on the approach to an interchange or through an interchange area where traffic is entering or exiting, or along deceleration or acceleration lanes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04a</span> <span class="ca">The TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-5.svg">R4-5</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) shall be used when a climbing lane is provided and it  is necessary to prohibit trucks from passing slower moving vehicles. Signs shall be placed at the beginning of the  restriction and at approximately 0.25 mile intervals. When the restriction is necessary during certain hours, the Specific  Hours/Day (R82aP(CA)) Plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10ca">Figure 2B-10(CA)</a>) shall be placed below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r53bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r53bca.webp">R53B(CA)</a> sign.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04b</span> <span class="ca">The TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-5.svg">R4-5</a>) sign may be placed to advise trucks that they must use the right lane except to  pass slow moving vehicles as provided in CVC 21654.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The TRUCKS USE RIGHT LANE sign may be used as a supplement to the SLOWER TRAFFIC KEEP RIGHT sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05a</span> <span class="ca">The TRUCKS OK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r70ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r70ca.webp">R70(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10ca">Figure 2B-10(CA)</a>) may be used to allow trucks to legally use a lane other than the  right lane or lanes to facilitate the safe and orderly movement of traffic, such as in advance of freeway branch connections, lane  drops, etc. If the TRUCKS OK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r70ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r70ca.webp">R70(CA)</a>) sign cannot fit on an overhead sign structure, the TRUCKS (R70A(CA)) and OK  (R70B(CA)) sign panels may be used in place of the TRUCKS OK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r70ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r70ca.webp">R70(CA)</a>) sign. Refer to CVC 21655.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05b</span> <span class="ca">The TRUCKS OK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r70ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r70ca.webp">R70(CA)</a>) sign shall be placed directly over the appropriate lane. The TRUCKS (R70A(CA)) and  OK (R70B(CA)) sign panels shall be placed on either side of and adjacent to the lower end of the appropriate white arrow.  See Figures 2B-10(CA) and 2E-36(CA).</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05c</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 21655</span>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If an extra lane has been provided for trucks and other slow-moving traffic, a Lane Ends sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2c/#2c47">Section 2C.47</a>) should be installed in advance of the point where the extra lane ends. Appropriate pavement markings should be installed at both the upstream and downstream ends of the extra lane (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b12">Section 3B.12</a> and <a href="#fig-3b-14">Figure 3B-14</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The TRUCKS 3 AXLES OR MORE RIGHT 2 LANES ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-3aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r6-3aca.webp">R6-3A(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10ca">Figure 2B-10(CA)</a>) may be used on  divided highways having four or more lanes for traffic in one direction where this type of vehicle, unless designated, is restricted to  the two right lanes. Refer to CVC 21655 and 22348(c).</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The ALL VEHICLES WHEN TOWING RIGHT 2 LANES ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-4aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r6-4aca.webp">R6-4A(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10ca">Figure 2B-10(CA)</a>) may be used on  divided highways having four or more lanes for traffic in one direction where this type of vehicle, unless designated, is restricted to  the two right lanes. Refer to CVC 21655 and 22348(c).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The END TRUCK LANE CONTROL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r53eca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r53eca.webp">R53E(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10ca">Figure 2B-10(CA)</a> shall be placed at the end of a segment  of roadway in which trucks are restricted to a particular lane.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The YIELD TO UPHILL TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r55ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r55ca.webp">R55(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-10ca">Figure 2B-10(CA)</a> shall be used facing downhill traffic where a  climbing lane has been provided and where a one-direction no passing marking has been placed to allow downhill traffic to  pass. Refer to CVC 21661. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b01">Section 3B.01</a> for further details.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b39" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.39 Keep Right and Keep Left Signs (R4-7 Series and R4-8 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.39 &middot; manual page 191 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.39/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used at locations where it is necessary for traffic to pass only to the right-hand side of a roadway feature or obstruction. The Keep Left (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used at locations where it is necessary for traffic to pass only to the left-hand side of a roadway feature or obstruction.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> At locations where it is not readily apparent that traffic is required to keep to the right, a Keep Right sign should be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If Keep Right signs are installed at the start of a median or at a median opening, they shall be placed as close as practicable to the approach ends of the medians, and shall be visible to traffic on the divided highway and angled toward the applicable crossroad approach as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the Keep Right sign should be mounted on the face of or just in front of a pier or other obstruction separating opposite directions of traffic in the center of the highway such that traffic will have to pass to the right-hand side of the sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Where the approach end of the island channelizes traffic away from the approach direction, the word legend (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7a.svg">R4-7a</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8a.svg">R4-8a</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8b.svg">R4-8b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) should be used instead of the symbol (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a>) signs to emphasize the degree of curvature away from the approach direction (see <a href="#fig-2b-11">Figure 2B-11</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Where a regulatory sign is used within the central island of a neighborhood traffic circle to direct traffic counter- clockwise around the central island, the Keep Right with diagonal arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>) sign should be used (see Figure 2B- 24). The mounting height of the sign should be at least 4 feet, measured vertically from the bottom of the sign to the elevation of the near edge of the traveled way.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-11"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0245.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0245.webp" alt="Figure 2B-11. Examples of Keep Right and Keep Left Sign Placement R4-7a R4-7 R4-7 R4-8b OR R4-7b R4-20 R4-7b R4-7 R4-7 Legend Direction of travel"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-11. Examples of Keep Right and Keep Left Sign Placement R4-7a R4-7 R4-7 R4-8b OR R4-7b R4-20 R4-7b R4-7 R4-7 Legend Direction of travel <span class="src-page">p 245</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Keep Right (Left) sign shall not be installed on the right-hand (left-hand) side of the roadway in a position where traffic must pass to the left-hand (right-hand) side of the sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The Keep Right sign may be omitted at intermediate ends of divisional islands and medians.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> Word message KEEP RIGHT (LEFT) with an arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7a.svg">R4-7a</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used instead of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a> symbol signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> A narrow Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7c.svg">R4-7c</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed on the approach end of a median island that is less than 4 feet wide at the point where the sign is to be located.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> A narrow Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7c.svg">R4-7c</a>) sign shall not be installed on a median island that has a width of 4 feet or more at the point where the sign is to be located.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> The Keep Right sign may <span class="ca">shall</span>be installed in the median of a divided highway crossing that functions as a single intersection such that it is visible to traffic on the divided highway and angled as needed toward the applicable crossroad approach as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">12a</span> <span class="ca">If the median is not wide enough to accommodate the KEEP RIGHT or narrow Keep Right sign, the sign may be omitted if all  other applicable standards are met.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b49">Section 2B.49</a> provides more information about the use of the Keep Right sign in combination with or in lieu of ONE-WAY signs at divided highway crossings.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b40" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.40 STAY IN LANE Sign (R4-9)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.40 &middot; manual page 192 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.40/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A STAY IN LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-9/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-9.svg">R4-9</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be used on multi-lane highways to direct road users to stay in their lane until conditions permit shifting to another lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If a STAY IN LANE sign is used, it should be accompanied by a solid double white lane line(s) to prohibit lane changing.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b41" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.41 RUNAWAY VEHICLES ONLY Sign (R4-10)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.41 &middot; manual page 192 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.41/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> A RUNAWAY VEHICLES ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-10.svg">R4-10</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) should <span class="ca">shall</span> be installed near a truck escape (or runaway truck) ramp entrance to discourage other road users from entering the ramp.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The NO STOPPING ANY TIME (R26A(S)(CA)) signs (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-25ca">Figure 2B-25(CA)</a>) shall be placed as required to keep  motorists from stopping in the path of runaway trucks.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-25ca"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0268.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0268.webp" alt="Figure 2B-25(CA). California Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs and Plaques"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-25(CA). California Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs and Plaques <span class="src-page">p 268</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Refer to <a href="#fig-3g-103ca">Figure 3G-103(CA)</a> for runaway truck signing.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b42" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.42 Slow Vehicle Turn-Out Signs (R4-12, R4-13, and R4-14)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.42 &middot; manual page 192 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.42/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> On two-lane highways in areas where traffic volumes and/or vertical or horizontal curvature make passing difficult, turn-out areas are sometimes provided for the purpose of giving a group of faster vehicles an opportunity to pass a slow- moving vehicle.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A SLOW VEHICLES WITH XX <span class="ca">5</span> OR MORE FOLLOWING VEHICLES MUST USE TURN-OUT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may <span class="ca">shall</span> be installed in advance of a <span class="ca">the</span><span class="ca">first</span>turn-out area to inform drivers who are driving so slow that they have accumulated a specific number of vehicles behind them that they are required by the traffic laws of that State to use the turn-out to allow the vehicles following them to pass. <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 21656.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The specific number of vehicles displayed on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign provides law enforcement personnel with the information they need to enforce this regulation.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 21656 for Turning out of Slow-Moving Vehicles.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">03b</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign is not intended to be used in advance of each individual turnout.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">03c</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b101ca">Section 3B.101(CA)</a> for more details.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign has been installed in advance of a turn-out area, a SLOW VEHICLES MUST USE TURN-OUT AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-13.svg">R4-13</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may also be installed downstream from the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign, but upstream from the turn-out area, to remind slow drivers that they are required to use a turn-out that is a short distance ahead.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-12.svg">R4-12</a> sign has been installed in advance of a turn-out area, a SLOW VEHICLES MUST TURN OUT (with arrow) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-14.svg">R4-14</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) shall be installed at the entry point of the turn-out area.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2d/#2d54">Section 2D.54</a> contains information regarding advance information signs for slow vehicle turn-out areas.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b43" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.43 DO NOT DRIVE ON SHOULDER Sign (R4-17) and DO NOT PASS ON SHOULDER Sign (R4-18)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.43 &middot; manual page 193 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.43/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The DO NOT DRIVE ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-17/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-17.svg">R4-17</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed to inform road users that using the shoulder of a roadway as a travel lane is prohibited.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The DO NOT PASS ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-18.svg">R4-18</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-10">Figure 2B-10</a>) may be installed to inform road users that using the shoulder of a roadway to pass other vehicles is prohibited.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b44" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.44 ALL TRAFFIC Sign (R4-20) and RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY Sign (R4-21)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.44 &middot; manual page 193 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.44/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The ALL TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-20.svg">R4-20</a>) sign may be used at an intersection where all traffic on the approach to the intersection must turn in the direction indicated and the Movement Prohibition (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b26">Section 2B.26</a>) and/or ONE WAY (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b49">Section 2B.49</a>) signs do not adequately convey the allowable direction of travel.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-21/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-21__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R4-21</a>) sign may be used at or on an approach to an intersection where all traffic on that approach must turn in the direction indicated.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY sign should not be used for a channelized turn lane separated from the adjacent travel lanes by an island.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The ALL TRAFFIC sign shall not be used to substitute for the Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> series) or Keep Left (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-8.svg">R4-8</a> series) signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The RIGHT (LEFT) TURN ONLY sign shall not be used to substitute for the Mandatory Movement Lane Control signs (see Sections 2B.27 and 2B.28).</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Selective Exclusion Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2b45" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.45 Selective Exclusion Signs and Plaques

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.45 &middot; manual page 194 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.45/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Selective Exclusion signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-12">Figure 2B-12</a>) may be used to provide notice to road users that State or local statutes or ordinances exclude designated types of traffic from using particular roadways or facilities.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Selective Exclusion signs shall clearly indicate the type of traffic that is excluded.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Typical exclusion messages include: A.No Trucks (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2.svg">R5-2</a>), B.NO MOTOR VEHICLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-3.svg">R5-3</a>), C.NO COMMERCIAL VEHICLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-4.svg">R5-4</a>), D.NO VEHICLES WITH LUGS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-5.svg">R5-5</a>), E.No Bicycles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-6.svg">R5-6</a>), F.NO NON-MOTORIZED TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-7.svg">R5-7</a>), G.NO MOTOR-DRIVEN CYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-8.svg">R5-8</a>), H.No Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a>), I. No Skaters (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-13.svg">R9-13</a>), J. No Equestrians (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-14.svg">R9-14</a>), K.No Snowmobiles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-15.svg">R9-15</a>), L.No All-Terrain Vehicles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-16.svg">R9-16</a>), M.Hazardous Material (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r14-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R14-3.svg">R14-3</a>) (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b67">Section 2B.67</a>), N.NO THRU TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-12.svg">R5-12</a>), O.NO THRU TRUCKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2b.svg">R5-2b</a>), P.EXCEPT ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-19p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-19P.svg">R9-19P</a>) plaque, and Q.EXCEPT LOCAL DELIVERY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2aP.svg">R5-2aP</a>) plaque.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Appropriate combinations or groupings of these legends into a single sign, such as NO PEDESTRIANS BICYCLES MOTOR-DRIVEN CYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10.svg">R5-10</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10a.svg">R5-10a</a>) or NO PEDESTRIANS OR BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10b.svg">R5-10b</a>), may be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If an exclusion is governed by vehicle weight, a Weight Limit sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b64">Section 2B.64</a>) should be used instead of a Selective Exclusion sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If used on a ramp to a freeway or expressway where pedestrian and bicyclist travel are prohibited by law or regulation, the NO PEDESTRIANS OR BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10b.svg">R5-10b</a>) <span class="ca">NO PEDESTRIANS BICYCLES MOTOR-DRIVEN CYCLES (R5- 10)</span> sign should be installed in a location where it is clearly visible to any pedestrian or bicyclist attempting to enter the limited access facility from a street intersecting the ramp. In locations where a freeway or expressway is accessed from a ramp from a roadway parallel to the freeway or expressway, the sign should be placed in a location that clearly indicates the prohibition applies only to the freeway or expressway or to the ramp.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Selective Exclusion sign should be placed on the right-hand side of the roadway at an appropriate distance from the intersection so as to be clearly visible to all road users turning into the roadway that has the exclusion. The NO PEDESTRIANS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10c.svg">R5-10c</a>) or No Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a>) sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b57">Section 2B.57</a>) should be installed so as to be clearly visible to pedestrians who are at a location where an alternative route is available.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The NO PEDESTRIANS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10c.svg">R5-10c</a>) or No Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a>) sign may also be used at underpasses or elsewhere where pedestrian facilities are not provided.</p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The NO THRU TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-12.svg">R5-12</a>) or NO THRU TRUCKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2b.svg">R5-2b</a>) signs may be used at locations to prohibit through traffic from using a particular roadway or facility.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The EXCEPT LOCAL DELIVERY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2aP.svg">R5-2aP</a>) plaque may be mounted below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2.svg">R5-2</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2b.svg">R5-2b</a> sign.</p>
