<p><span class="pn">11</span> The EXCEPT ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-19p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-19P.svg">R9-19P</a>) plaque may be used where such modes are allowed on a shoulder but not on the traveled way and placed at intersections with other roads and established paths or trails, where such vehicles or modes are expected to enter the highway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> The AUTHORIZED VEHICLES ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-11.svg">R5-11</a>) sign may be used at median openings and other locations to prohibit vehicles from using the median opening or facility unless they have special permission (such as law enforcement vehicles or emergency vehicles) or are performing official business (such as highway agency vehicles). <span class="ca">Restrictions on Use of Freeways </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> CVC Section 21960 authorizes Caltrans and local authorities, with respect to freeways under their respective jurisdictions, to  prohibit or restrict the use of freeways by pedestrians, bicycles or other non-motorized traffic or by any person operating a motor- driven cycle or a motorized bicycle.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Restrictions on use of a freeway shall be by the order of Caltrans, District Director.</p>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> No ordinance or resolution of local authorities shall apply to any State highway until the proposed ordinance or  resolution has been presented to, and approved in writing by, Caltrans.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> The District Directors have been delegated the authority to issue orders restricting the use of freeways. They are also authorized  to approve orders, ordinances or resolutions of local authorities, which would restrict the use of State highways.</p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> It is Caltrans’ policy to restrict the use of freeways when a satisfactory alternate route is available.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> The NO PEDESTRIANS BICYCLES MOTOR-DRIVEN CYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10.svg">R5-10</a>) sign shall be used on a freeway at or near the  beginning of the section of freeway to which the prohibition applies and on the right side of freeway entrance ramps.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> Prior to placement of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10.svg">R5-10</a> sign on State highways, an order signed by the Caltrans District Director should be on file.</p>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> At the end of freeway sections where both bicycles and pedestrians have been allowed, and on the continuing freeway where  such traffic is prohibited, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10.svg">R5-10</a> sign should be placed beyond the exit ramp gore.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10.svg">R5-10</a> sign may be modified by deleting the word BICYCLES at locations where bicycles are permitted on freeway  shoulders.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">22</span> The NO PEDESTRIANS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10c.svg">R5-10c</a>) sign shall be used at all freeways exit ramps to inform the public that pedestrians are  prohibited.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">23</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-10c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-10c.svg">R5-10c</a> sign should be placed on the left facing pedestrian traffic, which might enter a freeway exit ramp. The sign should  be placed up the ramp to avoid conflict with the ramp terminal signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">24</span> Where pedestrian problems exist, a second identical sign on the right-hand side of the offramp at the same location may be  installed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">25</span> Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2e/#2e59">Section 2E.59</a> for additional details.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">26</span> Refer to CVC 21101 through 21104, 22402 through 22405 and 35650 through 35755 for Truck Exclusion signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">27</span> The No Trucks (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2.svg">R5-2</a>) sign is used together with a Truck Exclusion (R20DP(CA) Series) plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-12ca">Figure 2B-12(CA)</a> to  specify the maximum width or other restrictions in effect.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">28</span> An alternative route should be evaluated for height, weight and size restrictions. Appropriate signs should be posted along the  route to advise motorists of any restrictions.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">29</span> Advance signs may be necessary to give trucks an opportunity to turn around and retrace their path or select another route.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">30</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-2.svg">R5-2</a> signs shall be placed at each end of the affected portion of a highway section. They shall be placed at a  distance of not more than 500 feet from the ends of an affected bridge or structure.</p>
<p><span class="pn">31</span> The Bridge Speed and Weight Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r21ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r21ca.webp">R21(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-12ca">Figure 2B-12(CA)</a> shall be used to specify the maximum  speed permitted on a bridge or structure for vehicles over a specified weight. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r21ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r21ca.webp">R21(CA)</a> sign shall not be erected more  than 500 feet in advance of the bridge or structure.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">32</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r21ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r21ca.webp">R21(CA)</a> sign, when used with the Weight Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r12-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R12-5.svg">R12-5</a>) sign, may be placed on the same post.</p>
<p><span class="pn">33</span> The Truck Length Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r20hca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r20hca.webp">R20H(CA)</a>) sign may be used at locations where a semi-truck over 65 feet in length and a semi-truck  with trailer over 75 feet in length is prohibited.</p>
<p><span class="pn">34</span> The No Trucks Variable Message (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r20-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r20-1ca.webp">R20-1(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-12ca">Figure 2B-12(CA)</a> may be used with an advance guide sign where  there is a truck restriction.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">35</span> The NEXT RIGHT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r20-1apca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r20-1apca.webp">R20-1aP(CA)</a>) Plaque (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-12ca">Figure 2B-12(CA)</a> shall be used below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r20-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r20-1ca.webp">R20-1(CA)</a> sign when no  advance guide sign is available.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">36</span> The AUTOS WITH TRAILERS -TRUCKS – PROHIBITED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r53dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r53dca.webp">R53D(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-12ca">Figure 2B-12(CA)</a> may be used at locations  where these vehicles are prohibited from using the roadway.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Do Not Enter, Wrong Way, One Way, And Related Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2b46" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.46 DO NOT ENTER Sign (R5-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.46 &middot; manual page 197 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.46/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">00</span> The DO NOT ENTER package is a DO NOT ENTER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1.svg">R5-1</a>) sign with a WRONG WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a>) sign directly beneath it on the  same sign assembly.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span> (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1.svg">R5-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) shall be used at the following locations: A.Where a two-way roadway becomes a one-way roadway (see <a href="#fig-2b-18">Figure 2B-18</a>); B.The intersection of an interchange exit ramp with a crossroad as specified in <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b48">Section 2B.48</a> (see <a href="#fig-2b-15">Figure 2B-15</a>); C.The intersection of a channelized or turning roadway with a two-way undivided crossroad; and D.Except as provided in Paragraph 4 of this Section, a<span class="ca">A</span>n intersection with a divided highway where the crossing functions as <span class="ca">a single (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-14ca">Figure 2B-14(CA)</a>) or</span> two separate intersections (see <a href="#fig-2b-14">Figure 2B-14</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline multi"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-13"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0248.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0248.webp" alt="Figure 2B-13. DO NOT ENTER, WRONG WAY, ONE WAY, and Related Signs and Plaques R6-5P R5-1 R5-1a R6-1 R6-2 R6-3 R6-3a R6-6 R6-7"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-13. DO NOT ENTER, WRONG WAY, ONE WAY, and Related Signs and Plaques R6-5P R5-1 R5-1a R6-1 R6-2 R6-3 R6-3a R6-6 R6-7 <span class="src-page">p 248</span></figcaption></figure><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-18"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0259.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0259.webp" alt="Figure 2B-18. Locations of ONE WAY Signs"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-18. Locations of ONE WAY Signs <span class="src-page">p 259</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b48">Section 2B.48</a> for DO NOT ENTER package requirements at interchange exit ramp terminals.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> A DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span>sign should be installed at other locations where additional emphasis is needed where wrong-way movements are prominent or where the intersecting angle of roadways is such that the visibility of ONE WAY signs alone does not sufficiently convey the restriction.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02a</span> <span class="ca">The DO NOT ENTER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1.svg">R5-1</a>) and WRONG WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a>) signs (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>), may be used as Activated Blank-Out  signs (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-13ca">Figure 2B-13(CA)</a>) for controlling reversible lanes and for prohibiting turns into reversible lanes.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02b</span> <span class="ca">If used for controlling reversible lanes and for prohibiting turns into reversible lanes, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1.svg">R5-1</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a> Activated  Blank-Out signs shall be used in two sets.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02c</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1.svg">R5-1</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a> Activated Blank-Out signs may also be used to supplement static <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1.svg">R5-1</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a> signs.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A DO NOT ENTER sign may be installed at an intersection with a divided highway where the crossing functions as a single intersection as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> A DO NOT ENTER sign may be omitted on a low-speed urban street that is a divided highway at a crossing that functions as two separate intersections.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> An EXCEPT BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7bP.svg">R3-7bP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used <span class="ca">below</span> with a DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span> sign when counter-flow bicycle traffic is allowed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span>sign, if used, should be placed directly in view of a road user at the point where a road user could wrongly enter a divided highway, one-way roadway, or ramp. The sign should be mounted facing traffic that might enter the roadway or ramp in the wrong direction.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> At a crossing with a divided highway that functions as a single intersection; the sign, if used, <span class="ca">package</span>should be placed on the outside edge side of the roadway facing traffic that might enter the roadway in the wrong direction.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> If the DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span>sign would be visible to traffic to which it does not apply, the sign should be turned away from, or shielded from, the view of that traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08a</span> <span class="ca">At least one set of the DO NOT ENTER package should be visible from each decision point on each likely wrong-way approach.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> A second DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span> sign may <span class="ca">shall </span>be used, particularly where traffic approaches from an intersecting roadway (see <a href="#fig-2b-14">Figure 2B-14</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> Section <span class="ca">2A.15 and</span> 2B.48 contains information regarding an optional lower mounting height for DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span>signs that are located along an exit ramp facing a road user who is traveling in the wrong direction.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a12">Section 2A.12</a> contains the provisions for the use of continuously-operated or actuated LEDs to enhance the conspicuity of signs.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b47" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.47 WRONG WAY Sign (R5-1a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.47 &middot; manual page 198 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.47/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The WRONG WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) may be used as a supplement to the DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span> sign where a crossroad intersects a one-way roadway in a manner that does not physically discourage or prevent wrong- way entry (see Figures 2B-14 and 2B-20).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the <span class="ca">A</span> WRONG WAY sign should <span class="ca">shall</span> be placed at a location along the one-way roadway farther from the crossroad than the DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span> sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b46">Section 2B.46</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The WRONG WAY sign should be placed on the same side of the road as the DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span>sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Section <span class="ca">2A.15 and</span> 2B.48 contains information regarding an optional lower mounting height for WRONG WAY signs that are located along an exit ramp facing a road user who is traveling in the wrong direction.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a12">Section 2A.12</a> contains the provisions for the use of continuously-operated or actuated LEDs to enhance the conspicuity of signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> For additional information for the WRONG WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r5-1a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R5-1a.svg">R5-1a</a>) sign, refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b46">Section 2B.46</a> and 2B.48.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b48" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.48 Wrong-Way Traffic Control at Interchange Ramps

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.48 &middot; manual page 198 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.48/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> At interchange exit ramp terminals where the ramp intersects a crossroad in such a manner that wrong-way entry could inadvertently be made, the following signs shall be used (see <a href="#fig-2b-15">Figure 2B-15</a>): A.At least one ONE WAY sign for each direction of travel on the crossroad shall be placed where the exit ramp intersects the crossroad. B.At least one DO NOT ENTER <span class="ca">package</span> sign shall be conspicuously placed near the downstream end of the exit ramp in positions appropriate for full view of a road user starting to enter wrongly from the crossroad. C.At least one <span class="ca">stand-alone</span> WRONG WAY sign shall be placed <span class="ca">upstream</span> on the exit ramp facing a road user traveling in the wrong direction.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The WRONG WAY sign may be mounted to the backside of an existing sign upstream the exit ramp facing a road user traveling  in the wrong direction.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> In addition, the following pavement markings should be used (see <a href="#fig-2b-15">Figure 2B-15</a>): A.On two-lane paved crossroads at interchanges, solid double yellow lines should be used as a center line for an adequate distance on both sides approaching the ramp intersections. B.Where crossroad channelization or ramp geometrics do not make wrong-way movements difficult, a lane-use arrow should be placed in each lane of an exit ramp near the crossroad terminal where it will be clearly visible to a potential wrong-way road user.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The following traffic control devices may be used to supplement the signs and pavement markings described in Paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Section: A.Additional ONE WAY signs may be placed, especially on two-lane rural crossroads, appropriately in advance of the ramp intersection to supplement the required ONE WAY sign(s). B.Additional WRONG WAY signs may be used. C.Slender, elongated wrong-way arrow pavement markings (see <a href="#fig-3b-21">Figure 3B-21</a> <span class="ca">and 3B-21(CA)</span>) intended primarily to warn wrong-way road users that they are traveling in the wrong direction may be placed upstream from the ramp terminus (see <a href="#fig-2b-15">Figure 2B-15</a>) to indicate the correct direction of traffic flow. Wrong- way arrow pavement markings may also be placed on the exit ramp at appropriate locations near the crossroad junction to indicate wrong-way movement. The wrong-way arrow markings may consist of pavement markings or bidirectional red- and-white raised pavement markers or other units that show red to wrong-way road users and white to other road users. D.Lane-use arrow pavement markings may be placed on the exit ramp and crossroad near their intersection to indicate the permissive direction of flow. E.Freeway entrance signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2d/#2d50">Section 2D.50</a>) may be used. F.Lane control signs or movement prohibition signs may be used on the approaches to the exit ramp. G.A Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7c.svg">R4-7c</a>) may be used on a ramp median nose for wrong-way traffic control.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> On interchange entrance ramps where the ramp merges with the through roadway and the design of the interchange does not clearly make evident the direction of traffic on the separate roadways or ramps, a ONE WAY sign visible to traffic on the entrance ramp and through roadway should be placed on each side of the through roadway near the entrance ramp merging point as illustrated in <a href="#fig-2b-16">Figure 2B-16</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-16"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0257.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0257.webp" alt="Figure 2B-16. Example of Application of Regulatory Signing and Pavement Markings at an Entrance Ramp Terminal"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-16. Example of Application of Regulatory Signing and Pavement Markings at an Entrance Ramp Terminal <span class="src-page">p 257</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> On interchange entrance ramps where the ramp merges with the through roadway and the design of the interchange does not clearly make evident the direction of traffic on the separate roadways or ramps a No Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>) sign may be located on the left-hand side of the entrance ramp at the gore (see Figure 2B- 16). If a No Left Turn (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>) sign is located on the left-hand side, a supplemental <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a> sign may be installed on the right-hand side of the entrance ramp.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> On interchange entrance ramps where the ramp merges with the through roadway and the design clearly indicates the direction of flow, a ONE WAY sign may be placed visible to traffic on the entrance ramp and/or a NO TURNS (R3- 3) sign may be placed visible to traffic on the entrance ramp and through roadway at the gore area as illustrated in <a href="#fig-2b-16">Figure 2B-16</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> Where there are no parked cars, pedestrian activity, or other obstructions such as snow or vegetation, and if an engineering study indicates that a lower mounting height would address wrong-way movements on freeway or expressway exit ramps, a DO NOT ENTER sign(s) and/or a WRONG WAY sign(s) that is located along the exit ramp at a location downstream <span class="ca">upstream</span>from the intersection with the crossroad facing a road user who is traveling in the wrong direction may be installed at a minimum mounting height of 3 feet, measured vertically from the bottom of the sign to the elevation of the near edge of the pavement. At the intersection with the crossroad, a WRONG WAY sign may be mounted at a minimum height of 3 feet on the same support on which a DO NOT ENTER sign is mounted at a height that complies with the provisions of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a15">Section 2A.15</a> (see <a href="#fig-2b-17">Figure 2B-17</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a15">Section 2A.15</a> for mounting heights of the DO NOT ENTER package.  In locations subject to deep snow, sign heights may be adjusted in accordance with engineering judgment.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Sections 2B.46, 2B.47, and 2B.49 contain further information on signing to avoid wrong-way movements at at- grade intersections on expressways.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The sign locations shown in <a href="#fig-2b-15ca">Figure 2B-15(CA)</a> are approximate.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-15ca"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0252.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0252.webp" alt="Figure 2B-15(CA). California Example of Application of Regulatory Signing and Pavement Markings at an Exit Ramp Termination to Deter Wrong-Way Entry"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-15(CA). California Example of Application of Regulatory Signing and Pavement Markings at an Exit Ramp Termination to Deter Wrong-Way Entry <span class="src-page">p 252</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> All ramp terminals should be reviewed under both day and night conditions by experienced signing personnel to determine exact  locations.  On-Ramp Terminal Signing</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> Lead-in signing directing motorists to on-ramps is important. Care should be taken to ensure that arrows on direction signs  couldn’t be interpreted as pointing into inappropriate roadways, especially off-ramp terminals.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Partial interchanges may need special attention with respect to lead-in signing. Trailblazing a route from a partial interchange to  another interchange may be necessary to ensure proper traffic movements.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> Freeway Entrance packages should be placed as near the diverge point between the on-ramp and the intersecting roadway as  practicable. The down diagonal arrow should always point toward the onramp pavement.</p>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Large Freeway Entrance signs should be used with the Freeway Entrance package unless proper placement requires the  smaller Freeway Entrance signs.  Off-Ramp Terminal Signing</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> The Turn Prohibition signs (Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b26">Section 2B.26</a>) shall be placed in suitable locations on the crossing street in  advance of the off-ramp.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="ca">The Do Not Enter packages should be placed at off-ramp terminals to meet the following criteria:  A. At least one package should be visible to a road user (within the scope of his headlights) at his decision point on each  potential approach.  B. At least one package should be in the head-on position for the road user turning into the off-ramp from each potential  approach. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> A field decision should be made on whether to use three Do Not Enter packages or four if the off-ramp is split by a traffic island.</p>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> The ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a>) signs should be placed as close to the crossing street as possible. If there are sidewalks immediately  adjacent to the cross street, these signs should be located behind the sidewalk to avoid conflicting with pedestrians.</p>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> At skewed ramp intersections, where the angle approaches 90°, a second ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a>) sign should be added on the  obtuse side when it would be visible to approaching traffic. Refer to <a href="#fig-2b-15ca">Figure 2B-15(CA)</a> Sheet 1.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b49" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.49 ONE WAY Signs (R6-1 and R6-2)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.49 &middot; manual page 200 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.49/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Except as provided in Paragraph 6 of this Section, t<span class="ca">T</span>he ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-2.svg">R6-2</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) shall be used to indicate streets<span class="ca">, ramp terminals,</span> or roadways upon which vehicular traffic is allowed to travel in one direction only.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">The ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-2.svg">R6-2</a>) sign may be used as an alternative to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> sign where space is limited and the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-2.svg">R6-2</a> sign is more  appropriate.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> ONE WAY signs shall be placed parallel to the one-way street at all alleys and roadways that intersect one- way roadways as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-18">Figure 2B-18</a>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> At the crossing of a roadway with a divided highway that functions as two separate intersections, ONE WAY signs shall be placed, visible to each crossroad approach, on the near right and far left corners of each intersection with the directional roadways (see <a href="#fig-2b-19">Figure 2B-19</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> At the crossing of a roadway with a divided highway that functions as a single intersection Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7.svg">R4-7</a>) signs (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b39">Section 2B.39</a>) and/or ONE WAY signs shall be installed (see <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>). If Keep Right signs are installed, they shall be placed as close as practicable to the approach ends of the medians and shall be visible to traffic on the divided highway and angled (as needed) toward the applicable crossroad approach as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-20">Figure 2B-20</a>. If ONE WAY signs are installed, they shall be placed on the near right and far left corners of the intersection and shall be visible to each crossroad approach.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">04b</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b18">Section 2B.18</a> when a ONE WAY sign is proposed on the same sign assembly as a STOP sign.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> At the crossing of a roadway with a divided highway, regardless of function as a single or separate intersections, ONE WAY signs may also be placed on the far right corner of the intersection as shown in Figures 2B-19 and 2B-20.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> ONE WAY signs may be omitted on the one-way roadways of divided highways, where the design of interchanges indicates the direction of traffic on the separate roadways.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> An EXCEPT BICYCLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-7bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-7bP.svg">R3-7bP</a>) plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-4">Figure 2B-4</a>) may be used with a ONE WAY sign when counter- flow bicycle traffic is allowed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b48">Section 2B.48</a> contains information for the placement of ONE WAY signs at a crossroad with an interchange.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> If used at unsignalized intersections with one-way streets, ONE WAY signs shall be placed on the near right and the far left corners of the intersection facing traffic entering or crossing the one-way street (see Figure 2B- 18).</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> If used at signalized intersections with one-way streets, ONE WAY signs shall be placed near the appropriate signal faces, on the poles holding the traffic signals, on the mast arm or span wire holding the signals, or at the locations specified for unsignalized intersections.</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> At unsignalized T-intersections where the roadway at the top of the T-intersection is a one-way roadway, ONE WAY signs shall be placed on the near-right and the far side of the intersection facing traffic on the stem approach (see <a href="#fig-2b-18">Figure 2B-18</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Where the central island of a roundabout allows for the installation of signs, ONE WAY signs may be used to direct traffic counter-clockwise around the central island (see Figures 2B-22 and 2B-23).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> Where used on the central island of a roundabout, the mounting height of a ONE WAY sign should be at least 4 feet, measured vertically from the bottom of the sign to the elevation of the near edge of the traveled way.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> The BEGIN ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-6.svg">R6-6</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) may be used to notify road users of the beginning point of a one direction of travel restriction on the street or roadway. The END ONE WAY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-7.svg">R6-7</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) may be used to notify road users of the ending point of a one direction of travel restriction on the street or roadway.</p>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> On streets and highways under local jurisdiction where medians are less than 30 feet wide, raised or unpaved, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> signs  may be placed in the median.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> The appropriate height of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> signs when placed in the median should be 1.5 feet.</p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> signs should also be placed parallel to the one-way street at the appropriate far corner to a wrong-way turn. They  should also be placed opposite the exits from alleys and other public ways.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> Authorities in charge of any highway may designate, by ordinance or resolution, any roadway, part of a roadway, or specific  lanes for one-way traffic. Refer to CVC 21657.</p>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> No such ordinance isa be effective with respect to a State highway until approved by Caltrans.</p>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> If, by local ordinance, a State highway through a city has been made one of a pair of one-way streets, the Commission may  adopt the additional street into the State Highway System. Refer to SHC § 111.5. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a05">Section 1A.05</a> for information regarding  this publication.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b50" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.50 Divided Highway Crossing Signs (R6-3 and R6-3a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.50 &middot; manual page 201 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.50/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> On unsignalized minor-street approaches from which both left turns and right turns are permitted onto a divided highway at a crossing that functions as two separate intersections (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a23">Section 2A.23</a>), except as provided in Paragraph 2 of this Section, a Divided Highway Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-3.svg">R6-3</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-3a.svg">R6-3a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) shall be used to advise road users that they are approaching an intersection with a divided highway (see <a href="#fig-2b-19">Figure 2B-19</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If the divided highway has a traffic volume of less than 400 AADT and a speed limit of 25 mph or less, at a crossing that functions as two separate intersections, the Divided Highway Crossing signs facing the unsignalized minor-street approaches may be omitted.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A Divided Highway Crossing sign may be used on signalized minor-street approaches from which both left turns and right turns are permitted onto a divided highway to advise road users that they are approaching an intersection with a divided highway.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If a Divided Highway Crossing sign is used at a four-leg intersection, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-3.svg">R6-3</a> sign shall be used. If used at a T-intersection, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-3a.svg">R6-3a</a> sign shall be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The Divided Highway Crossing sign shall be located on the near right corner of the intersection, mounted beneath a STOP or YIELD sign or on a separate support.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> An additional Divided Highway Crossing sign may be installed on the left-hand side of the approach to supplement the Divided Highway Crossing sign on the near right corner of the intersection.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b51" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.51 Roundabout Circulation Plaque (R6-5P)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.51 &middot; manual page 202 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.51/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Where the central island of a roundabout or neighborhood traffic circle does not provide a reasonable place to install a sign as provided elsewhere in this Chapter, Roundabout Circulation (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-5p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-5P.svg">R6-5P</a>) plaques (see <a href="#fig-2b-13">Figure 2B-13</a>) should be placed below the YIELD signs on each approach.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Paragraph 6 of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b39">Section 2B.39</a> contains information about the use of a Keep Right (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r4-7b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R4-7b.svg">R4-7b</a>) sign in the central island of a neighborhood traffic circle.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Paragraph 12 of <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b49">Section 2B.49</a> contains information about the use of a One Way (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-1__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R6-1</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r6-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R6-2.svg">R6-2</a>) sign in the central island of a roundabout.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> At roundabouts where ONE WAY signs have been installed in the central island, Roundabout Circulation plaques may be placed below the YIELD signs on approaches to roundabouts to supplement the central island signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The Roundabout Circulation plaque may be used at any type of circular intersection.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Examples of regulatory and warning signs for roundabouts and neighborhood traffic circles are shown in Figures 2B-21 through 2B-24.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Parking, Standing, Stopping, And Emergency Restriction Signs</div>


<span id="2b52" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.52 Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs (R7 and R8 Series)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.52 &middot; manual page 203 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.52/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Parking signs pertain to the parking, standing, and stopping of vehicles along the roadway and in designated parking areas. They cover a wide variety of regulations, and only general guidance can be provided here. The word “standing” when used on the R7 and R8 series of signs refers to the practice of a driver keeping the vehicle in a stationary position while continuing to occupy the vehicle. The word “stopping” when used on the R7 and R8 series signs refers to any vehicle, occupied by a driver or not, that stops other than to avoid conflict with other traffic or to comply with official direction. Other types of activities such as active loading, active passenger loading, and/or waiting might be established in State or local codes for use on R7 and R8 series <span class="ca">parking, standing, and stopping</span>signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">CVC Section 463 defines “parking”, which involves the standing of a vehicle, whether occupied or not. However, the temporary  loading or unloading of merchandise or passengers is not considered parking. CVC Section 587 defines “stopping”, which involves  the cessation of vehicle movement.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">01b</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 21112 through 22658 regarding the authorities for the various parking, stopping and loading signs.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Parking signs are categorized as either (1) prohibiting parking or (2) permitting parking with restrictions on how parking is allowed.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The types of parking, standing, or stopping prohibitions that might be encountered include, but are not limited to: A.Prohibited at all times; B.Prohibited only at certain times of the day and/or days of the week; C.Prohibited with exceptions, such as for bus stops, loading/unloading zones, persons with disabilities, or electric vehicle charging stations; or D.Prohibited under certain conditions, such as Snow Emergency Routes.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Permissive parking signs allowing parking with restrictions include, but are not limited to: A.Parking only allowed for limited time duration (such as 30 minutes or for 1 hour); B.Metered parking requiring payment at an individual or a multi-space parking meter, or through electronic means such as by telephone or mobile application.; C.Parking only for specific persons (such as those with disabilities or patrons or employees of a business) or specific vehicle types (such as electric vehicles, police/government vehicles, motorcycles, bicycles, or taxis); D.Angled or back-in angled parking when it is not commonly used in the area; E.Parking programs such as neighborhood/residential permits, school areas, or special events; and F.Emergency parking or stopping only.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for error in Paragraph 4, Item B text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details. Parking Regulations</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Parking on freeways which have full control of access and no crossing at grade may be prohibited under CVC § 21960.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Parking on other State highways may be restricted or prohibited under CVC §§ 22505 and 22506.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The Caltrans District Director is authorized to issue orders prohibiting or restricting the parking of vehicles on State highways.  The District Director is also authorized to approve ordinances or resolutions of local authorities prohibiting or restricting parking on  State highways.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The delegation of maintenance activities to local authorities is usually exercised under the authority of SHC § 130. Under a  proposal to delegate maintenance and parking regulation authority under CVC § 22506, Caltrans retains the authority to regulate  parking under the three conditions specified in CVC § 22505(a). The District Director of Transportation is authorized to make this  delegation of authority.  Policy on Parking Restrictions</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> No Stopping Any Time – Stopping should be prohibited at locations where the prohibition would reduce the risk of collisions or  where parking would unduly interfere with the movement of traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> No Parking Anytime – Parking should be prohibited at locations where the prohibition is necessary to accommodate other  activities and objectives, such as street sweeping, snow removal, public safety or preferential parking.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> Major factors that may be considered for No Stopping Anytime include:  • Narrow roadway width.  • Restricted visibility at intersections for pedestrian and vehicular traffic.  • Narrow shoulder width.  • Conversion of a parking lane to a through lane or right-turn lane.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> Limited Time Parking -Caltrans does not issue orders for limited time parking.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> On State highways, limited time parking restrictions may be initiated by local authorities and approved by Caltrans. Parking  prohibitions between certain hours may also be initiated by local authorities.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> Before time limit parking regulations are approved in rural areas, law enforcement agency shall be consulted.  Special Signs</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> The OK TO PARK ON BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r22ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r22ca.webp">R22(CA)</a>) sign may be used to inform motorists that parking is permitted on a bridge. Refer to  CVC 22500(k).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> The PARK PARALLEL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24ca.webp">R24(CA)</a>) sign should only be used where diagonal parking is prevalent, in violation of CVC 22502.</p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> If supported by local ordinance, the BACK-IN ANGLE PARKING ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24fca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24fca.webp">R24F(CA)</a>) sign should be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> The SCHOOL BUS ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24aca.webp">R24A(CA)</a>), TAXICAB ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24bca.webp">R24B(CA)</a>) and TOUR BUS  ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24cca.webp">R24C(CA)</a>) signs shall be used to inform motorists of location of stands for use by school buses,  taxicabs and tour buses, respectively. Refer to CVC 21112.</p>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> The MAIL DEPOSIT ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24dca.webp">R24D(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of curb restrictions at  locations for depositing mail in an adjacent mailbox. Refer to CVC 21458(a)(3)(B).</p>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> The BLOCK WHEELS TO CURB (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r24eca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r24eca.webp">R24E(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists when parking on a hill to block the  wheels of the vehicle by turning them against the curb, or by other means, when leaving it to stand unattended upon any  grade exceeding 3 percent. Refer to CVC 22509.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> The PARK OFF PAVEMENT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25ca.webp">R25(CA)</a>) sign may be used where it is likely that vehicles may stop on the traveled way and  interfere with through traffic. It may also be used as a temporary sign in snow areas where parking is permitted.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">22</span> The LOADING ONLY 7AM TO 6PM EXCEPT SUNDAY 30 MINUTE LIMIT w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25a-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25a-1ca.webp">R25A-1(CA)</a>) sign shall be  used to inform motorists of curb restrictions at locations for loading or unloading passengers or freight for the time as  specified by local ordinance. Refer to CVC 21458(a)(2).</p>
<p><span class="pn">23</span> The following signs shall be used to inform motorists of curb restrictions at locations for loading or unloading of  passengers for the time as specified by local ordinance. Refer to CVC 21458(a)(3)(A). • PASSENGER LOADING ONLY 5 MINUTE LIMIT w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25cca.webp">R25C(CA)</a>) sign  • School Passenger Loading ONLY 7AM TO 4PM SCHOOL DAYS 5 MINUTE LIMIT w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25d-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25d-1ca.webp">R25D-1(CA)</a>) sign  • PASSENGER LOADING ONLY 7AM TO 4PM SCHOOL DAYS 5 MINUTE LIMIT w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25eca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25eca.webp">R25E(CA)</a>) sign  • School Bus Passenger Loading ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25f-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25f-1ca.webp">R25F-1(CA)</a>) sign  • Tour Bus Passenger Loading ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25h-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25h-1ca.webp">R25H-1(CA)</a> sign</p>
<p><span class="pn">24</span> The CAR SHARE PARKING ONLY PERMIT REQUIRED w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r25jca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r25jca.webp">R25J(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to designate  certain streets or portions of streets for the exclusive parking privilege of vehicles participating in a car share vehicle  program or ridesharing program. Refer to CVC 22507.1.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">25</span> The NO PARKING ANY TIME with arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28ca.webp">R28(CA)</a>) or without arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26ca.webp">R26(CA)</a>) signs may be used to inform motorists of a  parking prohibition at a specific location. The NO PARKING ANY TIME with arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28aca.webp">R28A(CA)</a>) or without arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26aca.webp">R26A(CA)</a>) signs  may be used where a larger size is desirable.</p>
<p><span class="pn">26</span> CVC Section 21718 prohibits the stopping, parking or leaving of any vehicle upon a freeway. Large NO PARKING ANY TIME  (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26ca.webp">R26(CA)</a>) or EMERGENCY PARKING ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-4.svg">R8-4</a>) signs may be installed on freeways which have full control of access and no  crossing at grade to inform traffic that stopping, parking or leaving of any vehicle upon a freeway is prohibited.</p>
<p><span class="pn">27</span> The Tow-Away No Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26kca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26kca.webp">R26K(CA)</a>) sign may be used to inform motorists of a parking prohibition and tow-away zone at a  specific location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">28</span> The NO STOPPING FIRE LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26fca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26fca.webp">R26F(CA)</a>) sign may be used to inform motorists of a designated fire lane. Refer to CVC  22500.1.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">29</span> The NO STOPPING ANY TIME (R26(S)(CA)) sign or Tow-Away NO STOPPING ANY TIME (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26lca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26lca.webp">R26L(CA)</a>) sign shall be used  to inform motorists of a No Stopping Zone at a specific location where red curb marking is not used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">30</span> The NO PARKING HERE TO CORNER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26mca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26mca.webp">R26M(CA)</a>) sign shall be installed to inform motorists that parking is prohibited  within 20 feet of the vehicle approach side of a marked or unmarked crosswalk at a corner or within 15 feet of any marked  or unmarked crosswalk at a corner where a curb extension is present. Refer to CVC Section 22500.</p>
<p><span class="pn">31</span> For midblock crosswalk configurations, the NO PARKING HERE TO X-ING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26m-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26m-1ca.webp">R26M-1(CA)</a>) sign shall be used in place of  the standard <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26mca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26mca.webp">R26M(CA)</a> sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">32</span> The Tow-Away NO STOPPING 4 TO 6 PM (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r107ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r107ca.webp">R107(CA)</a>) sign in a tow-away zone shall be used to inform motorists of a  stopping restriction at a specific location during specific hours.</p>
<p><span class="pn">33</span> In zones where vehicles can be towed for violating the posted prohibition restriction, the “Tow-Away” message or  symbol shall be used. Where the “Tow-Away” symbol is used in a 12-inch width format, it shall be as shown in the  <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r26kca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r26kca.webp">R26K(CA)</a> sign. When it is used in an 18-inch width format, it shall be shown in the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r107ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r107ca.webp">R107(CA)</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">34</span> The NO STOPPING ANY TIME (R26A(S) (CA)) sign may be used where a larger size is desirable.</p>
<p><span class="pn">35</span> The NO PARKING ON BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r27ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r27ca.webp">R27(CA)</a>) sign may be used only where parking is prevalent in violation of CVC 22500(k).</p>
<p><span class="pn">36</span> The NO PARKING ON BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r27aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r27aca.webp">R27A(CA)</a>) sign may be used where a larger size is desirable.</p>
<p><span class="pn">37</span> The NO STOPPING ANY TIME with arrow (R28(S)(CA)) sign may be used to inform motorists of no stopping zones. Use only  where the beginning and ending points of the prohibition are not otherwise indicated. The NO STOPPING ANY TIME with arrow  (R28A(S)(CA)) sign may be used where a larger size is desirable.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">38</span> The NO STOPPING BUS ONLY w/ Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28cca.webp">R28C(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of no stopping zones at bus  stops.</p>
<p><span class="pn">39</span> On State highways, the NO PARKING VEHICLES OVER 6’ HIGH w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28dca.webp">R28D(CA)</a>) or NO STOPPING  VEHICLES OVER 6’ HIGH w/ Double Arrow (R28D(S)(CA)) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a parking or stopping  prohibition, which applies only to vehicles 6 feet or more in height. Refer to CVC 22505.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">40</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28dca.webp">R28D(CA)</a> or R28D(S)(CA)) sign may be installed within 100 feet of an intersection to improve the visibility of the motorists  in accordance with CVC 22507, except where parking would obstruct the applicable sight distance as determined by a qualified  engineer.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">41</span> The NO PUBLIC PARKING SUBJECT TO CITATION AND REMOVAL AT OWNER’S EXPENSE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28eca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28eca.webp">R28E(CA)</a>) sign shall be  used to inform motorists of a parking prohibition on private property. Refer to CVC 22658.</p>
<p><span class="pn">42</span> The No Parking VEHICLES OVER 5 TONS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r28fca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r28fca.webp">R28F(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a parking prohibition in  a residential district for commercial vehicles having a manufacturer's gross vehicle weight rating of 5 tons or more. Refer  to CVC 22507.5.</p>
<p><span class="pn">43</span> The No Stopping/No Parking Specific Hours (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r29ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r29ca.webp">R29(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a stopping and parking  prohibition during certain hours at a specific location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">44</span> The No Parking Specific Hours (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30ca.webp">R30(CA)</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30aca.webp">R30A(CA)</a>) signs shall be used to inform motorists of a parking restriction  during certain hours at a specific location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">45</span> The No Parking 10AM TO 12 PM WEDNESDAY STREET SWEEPING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30bca.webp">R30B(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists  of a parking prohibition for the purpose of street sweeping. Refer to CVC 22507.6.</p>
<p><span class="pn">46</span> The No Parking 2AM TO 6AM EXCEPT BY PERMIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30cca.webp">R30C(CA)</a>)or No Parking 2AM TO 6 AM CITYWIDE EXCEPT BY  PERMIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30dca.webp">R30D(CA)</a> sign shall be used to inform motorists of a parking prohibition between the hours of 2 a.m. and 6 a.m.  Refer to CVC 22507.5.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">47</span> When used, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30dca.webp">R30D(CA)</a> sign should be posted below the City Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/g9-5ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/g9-5ca.webp">G9-5(CA)</a>) sign or downstream from a freeway off-ramp.  Refer to CVC 22507.5.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">46</span> The No Parking w/Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30eca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30eca.webp">R30E(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a parking restriction at a specific  location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">47</span> The No Stopping Specific Hours School Days (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r30fca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r30fca.webp">R30F(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a stopping  prohibition during certain hours at a specific location in a school zone area.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">48</span> The No Parking/Parking Specific Hours (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r31ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r31ca.webp">R31(CA)</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r32bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r32bca.webp">R32B(CA)</a>) and No Stopping/Parking Specific Hours (R31(S)(CA)) signs  may be used to inform motorists of a stopping/parking prohibition during certain hours and a parking time limit during other hours at a  specific location. The R31(S)(CA) sign is used for stopping prohibitions, generally during peak traffic hours.</p>
<p><span class="pn">49</span> The Limited Hour/Minute Parking Specific Hours (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r32ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r32ca.webp">R32(CA)</a> sign may be used to inform motorists of a parking time limit with  specific hours and/or minutes during certain hours at a specific location.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">50</span> The 2 HOUR PARKING 8AM TO 6 PM DISTRICT 7 PERMITS EXEMPT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r32cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r32cca.webp">R32C(CA)</a>) sign or 30 MINUTE PARKING 2AM TO 6  AM DISTRICT 3 PERMITS EXEMPT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r32dca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r32dca.webp">R32D(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a parking time limit designation of  certain streets upon which preferential parking privileges are given to residents and merchants adjacent to the streets for  their use, under which the residents and merchants can be issued a permit or permits that exempt them from the  prohibition or restriction. Refer to CVC 22507.</p>
<p><span class="pn">51</span> A combined 2 HOUR PARKING 8AM TO 4 PM – PASSENGER LOADING ONLY 4PM TO MIDNIGHT 5 MINUTE LIMIT w/  Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r32eca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r32eca.webp">R32E(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of a parking time limit with specific hours and of curb  restrictions at locations for loading or unloading of passengers for the time as specified by local ordinance. Refer to CVC  21458(a)(3)(A).</p>
<p><span class="pn">52</span> The 2 HOUR PARKING 8AM TO 6 PM MOTORCYCLE PARKING ONLY w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r32fca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r32fca.webp">R32F(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to  inform motorists of a parking time limit with specific hours for motorcycles. Refer to CVC 22503.5.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">53</span> The Tow-Away No Stopping/No Parking Specific Hours (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r37ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r37ca.webp">R37(CA)</a>) sign may be used to inform motorists of no stopping and  parking prohibitions and tow-away zone at a specific location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">54</span> The Tow-Away No Parking/Limited Hour Parking Specific Hours (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r38ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r38ca.webp">R38(CA)</a>) sign may be used to inform motorists of a parking  restriction and tow-away zone at a specific location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">55</span> The Tow-Away No Stopping/Limited Hour Parking Specific Hours (R38(S)(CA)) sign may be used for stopping prohibitions,  generally during peak hours.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">56</span> Local agencies are allowed to adopt, by resolution or ordinance, the restriction of parking and the impounding of vehicles for  sale, subject to the requirements of CVC Section 22651.9. Under these requirements, a vehicle can be impounded if a parking  violation was received within the last 30 days and a warning was issued.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">57</span> The No Parking of Vehicles for Sale (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r108ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r108ca.webp">R108(CA)</a>) signs may be posted to inform motorists that the parking of vehicles for sale is  prohibited and that vehicles may be impounded, as prescribed in CVC Section 22651.9, and as authorized by a local ordinance or  resolution.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">58</span> If used, the applicable municipal code or county code should be shown on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r108ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r108ca.webp">R108(CA)</a> signs to assist enforcement personnel  in identifying the appropriate parking infraction, due to the special requirements prescribed in CVC Section 22651.9.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">59</span> The combined TOW-AWAY NO STOPPING 7AM TO 9 AM - PASSENGER LOADING ONLY ALL OTHER TIMES 5 MINUTE  LIMIT w/ Double Arrow (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r38aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r38aca.webp">R38A(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to inform motorists of no stopping and parking prohibitions and tow- away zone at a specific location during specific hours and of curb restrictions at locations for loading or unloading of  passengers for the time as specified by local ordinance. Refer to CVC 21458(a)(3)(A).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">60</span> The NO PARKING OF COMMERCIAL VEHICLES EXCEPT BY PERMIT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r39ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r39ca.webp">R39(CA)</a>) sign may be used on any roadway in which  local ordinance or resolution per CVC Section 22505 and 22507 has been established to prohibit parking of commercial vehicles.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">61</span> If used, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r39ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r39ca.webp">R39(CA)</a> sign shall be used to identify only those street or highway locations, either State or local, upon  which parking of commercial vehicles is prohibited, except by permit, as established by the local ordinance or resolution.</p>
<p><span class="pn">62</span> The NO DOUBLE PARKING ANYTIME COMMERCIAL VEHICLES INCLUDED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r39-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r39-1ca.webp">R39-1(CA)</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r39-2ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r39-2ca.webp">R39-2(CA)</a>) sign shall be  used to inform motorists of a parking prohibition in a business district for commercial vehicles where a local agency has  adopted an ordinance per CVC 22502(c).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">63</span> The NO IDLING COMMERCIAL VEHICLES AND ALL BUSES <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr62ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr62ca.webp">SR62(CA)</a> or NO IDLING All Buses and Commercial Vehicles  <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63ca.webp">SR63(CA)</a> symbol sign may be placed to remind commercial vehicle operators that idling is prohibited for commercial vehicles and all  buses for a duration greater than 5 minutes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">64</span> Refer to the CCR, Title 13, Division 3, Chapter 10, Article 1, §§ 2480 and 2485 which prohibits unnecessary idling of commercial  vehicles and all buses.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">65</span> If used, the NO IDLING COMMERCIAL VEHICLES AND ALL BUSES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr62ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr62ca.webp">SR62(CA)</a>) sign or NO IDLING All Buses and  Commercial Vehicles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63ca.webp">SR63(CA)</a>) symbol sign or NO IDLING All Buses and Commercial Vehicles REPORT VIOLATIONS  (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63aca.webp">SR63A(CA)</a>) sign shall be placed in areas where idling commonly occurs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">66</span> The NO IDLING All Buses and Commercial Vehicles REPORT VIOLATIONS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63aca.webp">SR63A(CA)</a>) sign may be used in areas with idling  concern for heavy-duty diesel trucks and buses to report violators directly to California Air Resources Board (CARB) or local law  enforcement agencies.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">67</span> The phone number used on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63aca.webp">SR63A(CA)</a> sign shall be either 1-800-CUT-SMOG or 1-800-END-SMOG, or another local  law enforcement agency’s number for signs located in the South Coast Mair Quality Management District. <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63aca.webp">SR63A(CA)</a> signs  located elsewhere in the State shall use the 1-800-END-SMOG or another local law enforcement agency’s number.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">68</span> Refer to Health and Safety Code, Division 26, Part 4, Chapter 5, Section 42705.5 and Health and Safety Code, Division 26, Part  6, Chapter 6, Section 44391.2.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">69</span> If using the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr63aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr63aca.webp">SR63A(CA)</a> sign, these signs should be placed within 100 feet of sensitive receptors, like daycares, school, senior  care facilities, hospitals, and residential neighborhoods.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">70</span> CCR Title 13, Sections 2480 and 2485, of the CCR prohibit unnecessary idling of commercial vehicles and all buses for a  duration greater than 5 minutes. The sign locations will be determined by Air Resources Board representatives and officials of the  law enforcement agency responsible for enforcement and the jurisdiction who owns the roadway will install the signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">71</span> The Accessible Parking Only (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a>) sign in combination with MINIMUM FINE $250 (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99bca.webp">R99B(CA)</a>) plaque; or,  Accessible Parking Only Minimum Fine $250 (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99cca.webp">R99C(CA)</a>) sign shall be used in on-street and off-street parking facilities to  designate stalls for vehicles with a special identification license plate or a distinguishing placard for persons with  disabilities.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">72</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a> sign in combination with the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99bca.webp">R99B(CA)</a> plaque; or, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99cca.webp">R99C(CA)</a> sign, blue pavement markings and International  Symbol of Accessibility Marking, are required for enforcement of these parking areas. Refer to CVC 22511.7 and 22511.8.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">73</span> The VAN ACCESSIBLE (R7-8b) sign shall be mounted below the Accessible Parking Only (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a>) sign in  combination with MINIMUM FINE $250 (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99bca.webp">R99B(CA)</a>) plaque; or, Accessible Parking Only Minimum Fine $250 (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99cca.webp">R99C(CA)</a>) sign  of the parking space for persons with disabilities designated as the van accessible space as provided in the Title 24, Part 2  California Building Code (CBC) Section 1129B.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
