<p><span class="pn">74</span> The TOW-AWAY SPECIAL PLACARD OR LICENSE PLATE REQUIRED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r100aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r100aca.webp">R100A(CA)</a>) sign may be used with the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a> sign  in combination with the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99bca.webp">R99B(CA)</a> plaque; or, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99cca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99cca.webp">R99C(CA)</a> sign to inform motorists that their vehicle will be towed away if they do not  have a special identification license plate or a distinguishing placard for persons with disabilities.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">75</span> The Disabled Tow-Away (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r100bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r100bca.webp">R100B(CA)</a>) sign shall be placed immediately adjacent to, and visible from, the stall or space,  or at each entrance to an off street parking facility to inform motorists that their vehicle will be towed away if they park in  designated stalls or spaces without a special identification license plate or a distinguishing placard for persons with  disabilities. The sign shall include the address where the towed vehicle can be reclaimed and the telephone number of the  local traffic law enforcement agency. Refer to CVC 22511.8 and 22511.9.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">76</span> The DISPLAY OF VEHICLES FOR SALE PROHIBITED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr26ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr26ca.webp">SR26(CA)</a>) sign may be used on any roadway in which local ordinance  or resolution per SHC, § 731 has been established to prohibit the display of vehicles for sale.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">77</span> If used, the SR26 (CA) sign shall be used to identify only those street or highway locations, either State or local, upon  which display of vehicles for sale is prohibited, as established by the local ordinance or resolution.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">78</span> The TOW-AWAY NO PARKING WHEN SNOW REMOVAL CONDITIONS EXIST (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr49ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr49ca.webp">SR49(CA)</a>) sign may be used to prohibit or  restrict the parking or standing of vehicles on designated streets or highways, or portions thereof, for the purpose of snow removal.  Refer to CVC 22510.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">79</span> The SNOW NOT REMOVED BEYOND HERE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr20-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr20-1ca.webp">SR20-1(CA)</a>) sign should be erected at the beginning of the snow season and  removed in the spring when the road is opened. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr20-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr20-1ca.webp">SR20-1(CA)</a> sign should be placed at a location that will provide a motorist the  opportunity to turn around.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">80</span> The CHAIN INSTALLATION ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r74ca.webp">R74(CA)</a>) sign may be erected where parked vehicles interfere with normal winter  operations.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">81</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r74ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r74ca.webp">R74(CA)</a> sign should be turned or covered at the end of the chain requirement season.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">82</span> The CHAINS REQUIRED (X MILE (X MILES)) AHEAD (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r75ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r75ca.webp">R75(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to give advance notice that chains are  required ahead.</p>
<p><span class="pn">83</span> The CHAINS REQUIRED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76ca.webp">R76(CA)</a>) sign shall be used at the beginning of chain control areas and intermittently as  needed.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">84</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76ca.webp">R76(CA)</a> sign is installed in combination with the Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>), <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r79ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r79ca.webp">R79(CA)</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r80-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r80-1ca.webp">R80-1(CA)</a> signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">85</span> The ON SINGLE AXLE DRIVE VEHICLE WITH TRAILER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76-1ca.webp">R76-1(CA)</a>) sign may be used when road conditions are such that  only single drive vehicles with trailers need chains.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">86</span> When used, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76-1ca.webp">R76-1(CA)</a> sign shall be mounted below the CHAINS REQUIRED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76ca.webp">R76(CA)</a>) sign.</p>
<p><span class="pn">87</span> The NO EXCEPTIONS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r77ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r77ca.webp">R77(CA)</a>) sign shall be used with the Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>) and CHAINS REQUIRED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76ca.webp">R76(CA)</a>) signs  when chains are required with no exceptions.</p>
<p><span class="pn">88</span> The END CHAIN CONTROL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r78ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r78ca.webp">R78(CA)</a>) sign shall be used to advise the motorist that the chain control area has ended.</p>
<p><span class="pn">89</span> The AUTOS &amp; PICKUPS SNOW TIRES OK – CARRY CHAINS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r79ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r79ca.webp">R79(CA)</a>) sign shall be used with the Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>)  and CHAINS REQUIRED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76ca.webp">R76(CA)</a>) signs when chains are required but autos and pickups with snow tires are excepted from  using chains.</p>
<p><span class="pn">90</span> The 4-W DRIVE WITH SNOW TIRES OK – CARRY CHAINS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r80-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r80-1ca.webp">R80-1(CA)</a>) sign shall be used with the Speed Limit (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r2-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R2-1.svg">R2-1</a>)  and CHAINS REQUIRED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r76ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r76ca.webp">R76(CA)</a>) signs when chains are required.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">91</span> Vehicles with four wheel drive and snow tires on all four wheels are exempt from using chains.  Electric Vehicle Charging Station Signs</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">92</span> If used, the Electric Vehicle Charging Station Tow-Away (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r112ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r112ca.webp">R112(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-25ca">Figure 2B-25(CA)</a>) shall be placed  immediately adjacent to, and visible from, the charging station stall or space, or at each entrance to an off-street parking  facility to inform motorists that their vehicles will be towed away if parked in designated stalls or spaces without being  connected for electric charging purposes. The sign shall include the address where the towed vehicle can be reclaimed and  the telephone number of the local traffic law enforcement agency. Refer to CVC 22511.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">93</span> Local agencies may, at their discretion, include CVC Section 22511 or local municipal code section, or ordinance number on the  Electric Vehicle Charging Station Tow-Away <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r112ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r112ca.webp">R112(CA)</a> sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">94</span> If used, the No Parking (symbol) EXCEPT FOR EV CHARGING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r113ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r113ca.webp">R113(CA)</a>), or the No Parking (symbol) EXCEPT FOR  ELECTRIC VEHICLE CHARGING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r113aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r113aca.webp">R113A(CA)</a> sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-25ca">Figure 2B-25(CA)</a>) shall be placed immediately adjacent to, and  visible from, each charging station stall or space.</p>
<p><span class="pn">95</span> If used, the __HOUR EV CHARGING __AM TO __PM (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r114ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r114ca.webp">R114(CA)</a>), or the __HOUR ELECTRIC VEHICLE CHARGING __AM  TO __PM (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r114aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r114aca.webp">R114A(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-25ca">Figure 2B-25(CA)</a>) shall be placed immediately adjacent to, and visible from, each  charging station stall or space to identify the allowable time limit where electric vehicles may be connected, depending  upon what time limitations for charging apply to each charging station.</p>
<p><span class="pn">96</span> Identification signs and surface markings shall be placed for Van Accessible, Standard Accessible and Ambulatory  electric vehicle charging stations, as required by Chapter 11B of the Title 24, Part 2 California Building Code (CBC) Section  1129B. The International Symbol of Accessibility for the Handicapped (D9-6) sign in combination with the VAN  ACCESSIBLE (R7-8b) sub plaque shall be placed at Van Accessible electric vehicle charging stations, or, the D9-6 sign  shall be placed at standard accessible electric vehicle charging stations (refer to CBC 11B-812.8; and, <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/3b/#3b20">Section 3B.20</a> for  surface markings).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">97</span> The Rest Area/Vista Point 8 HOUR PARKING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/s23ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/s23ca.webp">S23(CA)</a>) sign may be used to discourage extended stays in roadside rests or  vista points for noncommercial vehicles. The 10 HOUR PARKING COMMERCIAL MOTOR VEHICLES (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r39-3ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r39-3ca.webp">R39-3(CA)</a>) may be used to  allow ten total hours of parking for commercial vehicles. Refer to CVC 22651(s)1 and CVC 22651(s)2.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b53" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.53 Design of Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.53 &middot; manual page 209 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.53/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Parking, standing<span class="ca">loading</span>, or stopping signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) shall be rectangular or square.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Public agencies shall follow established law (State law, local ordinance, or regulation) as adopted by the authorized agency regarding what messages are allowed on parking signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The legend on parking signs shall state applicable regulations. Parking signs shall comply with the standards of shape, color, and location.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The colors in parking signs shall conform to their associated curb zone colors.Prohibitive parking signs (see Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a> for some commonly used examples) shall be used where parking is prohibited at all times or at specific times. Except as otherwise provided in this Section, parking signs shall have a red legend and border on a white background and, when the parking prohibition symbol is used, the symbol “P” shall be black. <span class="ca">Where an exclusive zone is established for freight or passenger loading or mail deposit, the signs shall have a black  legend on a white background. Where an exclusive zone is established for disabled persons, the signs shall have a white  legend on a blue background, as shown on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a>.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> Permissive parking signs (see Drawing B in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) shall be used where only time-limited parking or parking in a particular manner is allowed. Permissive parking signs shall have a green legend and border on a white background.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-25"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0266.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0266.webp" alt="Figure 2B-25. Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs and Plaques (R7 and R8 Series)"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-25. Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs and Plaques (R7 and R8 Series) <span class="src-page">p 266</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Parking information, should be displayed from top to bottom of the sign, as applicable, in the following order: <span class="ca">A0. Any tow-away message or symbol. </span> A.The restriction or prohibition; B.The times of the day that it is applicable, if not all hours; C.The days of the week that it is applicable, if not every day; D.Qualifying or supplementary information; E.Exemptions to the restriction of prohibition; and F.Any tow-away message or symbol. <span class="ca">G. The appropriate municipal or county code on selected signs, when deemed necessary in order to aid enforcement  personnel in identifying the appropriate infraction.  H. The phone number to call to recover an impounded or towed vehicle. </span></p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> If the parking regulation applies to a limited area or zone, the limits of the regulation should be shown by arrows or supplemental plaques. If arrows are used and ifthe sign is at the end of a parking zone, there should be<span class="ca">either no arrows or</span> a single-headed arrow pointing in the direction that the regulation is in effect. If the sign is at an intermediate point in a zone, there should be a double-headed arrow pointing both ways. When a single sign is used at the transition point between two parking zones, it should display a right arrow and a left arrow pointing in the direction that the respective regulations apply.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The times and days for which the parking regulations are in effect shall be posted if they are not in effect at all times of day or all days of the week.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> As an alternate to the use of arrows to show designated restriction zones, the following word messages may be used: BEGIN, END, HERE TO CORNER, HERE TO ALLEY, and THIS SIDE OF SIGN.</p>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The R8 series signs (see Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) may be used where sufficient notice of a parking prohibition is satisfied by the use of single signs and are not needed to designate the beginning and end of a zone in which parking is prohibited or restricted. In rural and certain other areas the legends NO PARKING ON PAVEMENT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-1.svg">R8-1</a>) or NO STOPPING ON PAVEMENT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-5.svg">R8-5</a>) are generally suitable and may be used where parking or stopping is allowed on an unpaved shoulder or border adjacent to the paved portion of the road. If a roadway has an adjacent paved shoulder on which parking or stopping is allowed, the legend NO PARKING EXCEPT ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-2.svg">R8-2</a>) or NO STOPPING EXCEPT ON SHOULDER (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-6.svg">R8-6</a>) may be used. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-3.svg">R8-3</a> symbol sign or the word message NO PARKING may be used to prohibit any parking along a roadway. Word legend supplemental plaques may be mounted below the NO PARKING signs or the word legend may be incorporated within signs whose sizes are increased accordingly. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-3.svg">R8-3</a> series signs may include word legends such as ON PAVEMENT (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-3c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-3c.svg">R8-3c</a>), ON BRIDGE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-3d/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-3d.svg">R8-3d</a>), ON TRACKS (R8- 3e), and EXCEPT ON SHOULDERS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-3f/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-3f.svg">R8-3f</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> Where special parking restrictions are imposed during heavy snowfall or a declared snow emergency, a Snow Emergency Route (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-203/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-203.svg">R7-203</a>) sign (see Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) should be installed. The legend should be modified to display the specific regulations. The upper section of the sign should display the designation as a snow emergency route in a white legend and border on a red background.</p>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> If a fee is charged for on-street parking and payments are made at a multi-space parking meter, instead of individual parking meters for each parking space, Metered Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-21/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-21.svg">R7-21</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-22/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-22.svg">R7-22</a>) signs <span class="ca">and PAY AT STATION  (R109P(CA)) Plaque</span>(see Drawing B in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) should be used to define the area where the multi-space parking meter applies. The Multi-Space Parking Meter (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-20.svg">R7-20</a>) sign (see Drawing B in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) should be used at the meter location to direct road users to the meter.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> Where payments can be made electronically, such as by telephone or mobile application, the Mobile Parking Payment (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-21ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-21aP.svg">R7-21aP</a>) plaque (see Drawing B in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) may be installed below or as part of the legend of a Metered Parking sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> If the metered parking is subject to a maximum time limit, the appropriate time limit (number of hours or minutes) shall be displayed on the Metered Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-21/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-21.svg">R7-21</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-22/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-22.svg">R7-22</a>) signs and, except as provided in Paragraph 15 of this Section, on the Multi-space Parking Meter (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-20.svg">R7-20</a>) signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> Where the maximum time limit varies by the time of the day or by the day of the week, the display of the time limits may be omitted from the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-20/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-20.svg">R7-20</a> sign and, instead, be displayed on the multi-space parking meter so that they are visible to pedestrians as they make payments.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> Where parking spaces are reserved for persons with disabilities, the Accessible Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-8.svg">R7-8</a> <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a></span>) sign (see Drawing D in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a><span class="ca">(CA)</span>) shall be used to designate the space and shall display the official International Symbol of Accessibility.</p>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> Where parking spaces that are reserved for persons with disabilities are designed to accommodate wheelchair vans, a VAN ACCESSIBLE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-8ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-8aP.svg">R7-8aP</a><span class="ca"> R7-8bP(CA</span><span class="ca">)</span>) plaque (see Drawing D in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) shall be mounted below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-8.svg">R7-8</a> <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a></span> sign. <span class="ca">Refer to CCR Title 24, § 1129B.4.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">17a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b52">Section 2B.52</a> for the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r99ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r99ca.webp">R99(CA)</a> sign and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-8bpca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r7-8bpca.webp">R7-8bP(CA)</a> plaque.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> Where parking spaces are designated for parking of electric vehicles, an Electric Vehicle Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-111/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-111.svg">R7-111</a> series, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-112/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-112.svg">R7-112</a> series, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-113/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-113.svg">R7-113</a>) sign (see Drawing E of <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) should be installed adjacent to the designated spaces. Where there is no time limit, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-111/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-111.svg">R7-111</a> series sign should be used. Where parking is subject to a time limit, the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-112/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-112.svg">R7-112</a> series sign should be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">19</span> Where parking spaces are only designated for charging of electric vehicles, an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-113/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-113.svg">R7-113</a> sign or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-114/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-114.svg">R7-114</a> series sign (see Drawing E in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) should be installed adjacent to the designated spaces.</p>
<p><span class="pn">20</span> Where additional restrictions apply while a vehicle occupies the designated space, the R7-113P series plaques should be installed below the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-113/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-113.svg">R7-113</a> sign or the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-114/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-114.svg">R7-114</a> series signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">21</span> Where parking is prohibited during certain hours and time-limited parking or parking in a particular manner is allowed during certain other time periods, the red Parking Prohibition and green Permissive Parking signs may be designed as follows (see Drawing C in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>): A.Two 12 x 18-inch parking signs may be used with the red Parking Prohibition (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-1.svg">R7-1</a>) sign installed above or to the left of the green Permissive Parking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-108/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-108.svg">R7-108</a>) sign; or B.A single sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-200/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-200__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R7-200</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-200a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-200a__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R7-200a</a>) may be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">22</span> At the transition point between two parking zones, a single sign (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-200/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-200__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R7-200</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-200a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-200a__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R7-200a</a>) or two signs mounted side-by- side may be used.</p>
<p><span class="pn">22a</span> <span class="ca">On any sign, the words “Tow-Away” may be used interchangeably with the Tow-Away symbol.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">23</span> On any sign, the The words NO PARKING may be used as an alternative to the No Parking symbol (see the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-2a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-2a.svg">R7-2a</a> sign in Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">24</span> Alternate designs for the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-107/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-107.svg">R7-107</a> sign may be developed such as the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-107a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-107a.svg">R7-107a</a> sign (see Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>). Alternate designs may include, on a single sign, a transit logo, an approved bus symbol, a parking prohibition, the words BUS STOP, and an arrow. The reverse side of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-107/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-107.svg">R7-107</a> series signs may display bus routing information for pedestrians.</p>
<p><span class="pn">25</span> A Tow-Away Zone (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-201p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-201P.svg">R7-201P</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-201ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-201aP.svg">R7-201aP</a>) plaque (see Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>) may be mounted below any parking prohibition sign. The word legend TOW-AWAY ZONE may be incorporated into the parking prohibition sign in lieu of using a separate plaque.</p>
<p><span class="pn">26</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-201p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-201P.svg">R7-201P</a> plaque may have a black or red symbol and border on a white background.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">27</span> When a legend other than that on the standard parking signs is necessary, letter height, symbol size, and basic sign layout should be consistent with the those shown on the standard parking signs as detailed in the “Standard Highway Signs” publication (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a05">Section 1A.05</a>.)</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">27a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for errors in Paragraph 27 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">28</span> In general, the letter height of the principal legend on parking signs sized for urbanized applications should be at least 2 inches.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b54" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.54 Placement of Parking, Standing, and Stopping Signs

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.54 &middot; manual page 212 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.54/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The efficacy of parking, standing, and stopping signs, when used on conventional roads in urbanized or developed environments, depends on their visibility and consistent placement along a street or within a particular block. It is often impracticable for the entire legend to be legible from similar distances as for other types of signs. Therefore, it is important that their conventional form be recognizable from an adequate distance such that the road user can obtain the information upon closer inspection.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> When signs with arrows are used to indicate the extent of the restricted zones, the signs should be set at an angle of not less than 30 degrees nor more than 45 degrees with the line of traffic flow in order to be visible to approaching traffic.</p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> When signs are placed at the head of perpendicular parking stalls, the signs should be parallel to the roadway facing the parking stall.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> Spacing of signs should be based on legibility(<span class="ca">refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2a/#2a08">Section 2A.08</a>)</span>, conspicuity, and sign orientation(<span class="ca">refer to Section  2A.17)</span>.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> If the zone is long, signs should be used at intermediate points within the zone.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If the signs are mounted at an angle of 90 degrees to the curb line, two signs should be mounted back to back at the transition point between two parking zones, each with an appended THIS SIDE OF SIGN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r7-202p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R7-202P.svg">R7-202P</a>) supplemental plaque (see Drawing A in <a href="#fig-2b-25">Figure 2B-25</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> If the signs are mounted at an angle of 90 degrees to the curb line, signs without any arrows or appended plaques should be used at intermediate points within a parking zone, facing in the direction of approaching traffic. Otherwise, the standards of placement should be the same as for signs using directional arrows.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Blanket parking regulations that apply to an entire jurisdiction may, if legal, be posted in the vicinity of the jurisdictional boundary lines. Blanket parking regulations that apply to a posted zone or district may, if legal, be posted at the entry points to the zone or district.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b55" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.55 Emergency Restriction Signs (R8-4 and R8-7)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.55 &middot; manual page 212 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.55/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Emergency Restriction signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-26">Figure 2B-26</a>) shall be rectangular and shall have a black legend and border on a white background.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-26"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0271.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0271.webp" alt="Figure 2B-26. Emergency Restriction Signs R8-4 R8-7"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-26. Emergency Restriction Signs R8-4 R8-7 <span class="src-page">p 271</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The EMERGENCY PARKING ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-4.svg">R8-4</a>) sign or the EMERGENCY STOPPING ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-7.svg">R8-7</a>)sign may be used to discourage or prohibit shoulder parking, particularly where scenic or other attractions create a tendency for road users to desire to stop temporarily.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/8b/#8b07">Section 8B.07</a> contains information for the use of the DO NOT STOP ON TRACKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-8.svg">R8-8</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-8b-1">Figure 8B-1</a>) to discourage or prohibit parking or stopping on railroad or light rail transit tracks.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The EMERGENCY PARKING ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r8-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R8-4.svg">R8-4</a>) sign shall be used at the beginning of freeways below the BEGIN FREEWAY  (R57(CA)) sign. Refer to CVC 21960.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> See <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2b/#2b109ca">Section 2B.109(CA)</a> for the BEGIN/END FREEWAY (R57(CA) and R58(CA)) sign.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Pedestrian Signs</div>


<span id="2b56" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.56 WALK ON LEFT FACING TRAFFIC and No Hitchhiking Signs (R9-1, R9-4, and R9-4a)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.56 &middot; manual page 213 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.56/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The WALK ON LEFT FACING TRAFFIC (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-1.svg">R9-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-27">Figure 2B-27</a>) may be used on highways where no sidewalks are provided.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-27"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0272.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0272.webp" alt="Figure 2B-27. Pedestrian Signs and Plaques"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-27. Pedestrian Signs and Plaques <span class="src-page">p 272</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the WALK ON LEFT FACING TRAFFIC sign should be installed on the right-hand side of the road where pedestrians walk on the pavement or shoulder in the absence of pedestrian pathways or sidewalks.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The No Hitchhiking (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-4.svg">R9-4</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-27">Figure 2B-27</a>) may be used to prohibit standing in or adjacent to the roadway for the purpose of soliciting a ride. The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-4a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-4a.svg">R9-4a</a> word message sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-27">Figure 2B-27</a>) may be used as an alternate to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-4.svg">R9-4</a> symbol sign.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b57" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.57 Pedestrian Crossing Signs (R9-2 and R9-3)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.57 &middot; manual page 213 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.57/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Pedestrian Crossing signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-27">Figure 2B-27</a>) may be used to limit pedestrian crossing to specific locations.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, Pedestrian Crossing signs shall be installed to face pedestrian approaches.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Where crosswalks are clearly defined, the CROSS ONLY AT CROSSWALKS (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-2.svg">R9-2</a>) sign may be used to prohibit pedestrians from crossing at locations away from crosswalks.</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The No Pedestrian Crossing (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a>) sign may be used to prohibit pedestrians from crossing a roadway at an undesirable location or in front of a school or other public building where a crossing is not designated.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The NO PEDESTRIAN CROSSING (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3a.svg">R9-3a</a>) word message sign may be used as an alternate to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a> symbol sign. The USE CROSSWALK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3bP.svg">R9-3bP</a>) supplemental plaque, along with an arrow, may be installed below either sign to designate the direction of the crossing.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05a</span> <span class="ca">The NO PED CROSSING - USE CROSSWALK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r49ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r49ca.webp">R49(CA)</a>) Sign may be used as an alternate to the combined <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3.svg">R9-3</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3bP.svg">R9-3bP</a>  signs.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">05b</span> <span class="ca">The NO PED CROSSING - USE CROSSWALK (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r49ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r49ca.webp">R49(CA)</a>) Sign shall only be mounted on a Pedestrian Barricade.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Pedestrians with vision disabilities might need features other than traffic control devices to provide effective communication of the prohibition of pedestrian crossing.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r9-3bp/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R9-3bP.svg">R9-3bP</a> plaque should not be installed in combination with educational plaques.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Refer to CVC 21106.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b58" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.58 Traffic Signal Pedestrian and Bicyclist Actuation Signs (R10-1 through R10-4 and R10-24 through R10-26)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.58 &middot; manual page 213 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.58/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Where manual actuation of a traffic signal is required for pedestrians or bicyclists to call a signal phase to cross the roadway, traffic signal signs applicable to pedestrian actuation (see Figure 2B- 27) or bicyclist actuation (see <a href="#fig-9b-1">Figure 9B-1</a>) shall be mounted immediately above or incorporated into the push button detector units (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4i/#4i05">Section 4I.05</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Traffic signal signs applicable to pedestrians include: A.CROSS ONLY ON GREEN (symbolic circular green) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-1.svg">R10-1</a>), B.CROSS ONLY ON (symbolic walk indication) SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-2.svg">R10-2</a>), C.Push Button for Walk Signal (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3.svg">R10-3</a> series), and D.Push Button for Green Signal (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-4.svg">R10-4</a> series).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The following signs may be used as an alternate for the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3.svg">R10-3</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-4.svg">R10-4</a> signs: A.Push Button to Cross Street Wait for Walk Signal (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3a.svg">R10-3a</a>); or B.Push Button to Cross Street Wait for Green Signal (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-4a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-4a.svg">R10-4a</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The name of the street to be crossed may be substituted for the word STREET in the legends on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3a.svg">R10-3a</a> and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-4a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-4a.svg">R10-4a</a> signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The finger in the push button symbol on the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3.svg">R10-3</a>, <span class="ca">and</span> <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3a.svg">R10-3a</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-4.svg">R10-4</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-4a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-4a.svg">R10-4a</a> signs should point in the same direction as the arrow on the sign.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> Where symbolic pedestrian signal indications are used, an educational sign (R10-3b) may be used instead of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-3.svg">R10-3</a> sign to improve pedestrian understanding of pedestrian indications at signalized intersections. Where word- legend pedestrian signal indications are being retained for the remainder of their useful service life, the legends WALK/DONT WALK may be substituted for the symbols on the educational sign R10-3b, thus creating educational sign R10-3c. The R10-3d educational sign may be used to inform pedestrians that the pedestrian clearance time is sufficient only for the pedestrian to cross to the median at locations where pedestrians cross in two stages using a median refuge island. The R10-3e educational sign may be used where countdown pedestrian signals have been provided. In order to assist the pedestrian in understanding which push button to push, the R10-3f through R10-3i educational signs that provide the name of the street to be crossed may be used instead of the R10-3b through R10-3e educational signs.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06a</span> <span class="ca">The R10-3e(CA) or R10-3i(CA) educational sign may be used where countdown pedestrian signals have been provided.  Support</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">06b</span> <span class="ca">Pedestrian pushbuttons or touch-free detectors may be used to actuate pedestrian signal timing, to activate accessible  pedestrian signals, or both. Refer to Chapter 4K regarding the application of accessible pedestrian signals and detectors.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06c</span> <span class="ca">The R10-3j(CA) sign may be used where the pedestrian signal can be activated by pushing the button or waving at the button.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">06d</span> <span class="ca">The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-3kca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r10-3kca.webp">R10-3k(CA)</a> sign may be used where warning lights or rapid flashing beacons can be activated by pushing the button or  waving at the button.  Standard</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">06e</span> <span class="ca">The bottom panels of signs R10-3e(CA) and R10-3i(CA) shall not be used where the pedestrian signal timing is non- actuated and the pedestrian push button is used solely to activate accessible pedestrian signals.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-24/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-24.svg">R10-24</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-26/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-26.svg">R10-26</a> sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/9b/#9b20">Section 9B.20</a>) may be used where a push button detector has been installed exclusively to actuate a green phase for bicyclists.</p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-25.svg">R10-25</a> sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-27">Figure 2B-27</a>) may be used where a push button detector has been installed for pedestrians to activate In-Roadway Warning Lights (see Chapter 4U) or flashing beacons that have been added to the pedestrian warning signs.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4u/#4u02">Section 4U.02</a> for standards related to the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-25/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-25.svg">R10-25</a> sign.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4i/#4i05">Section 4I.05</a> contains information regarding the application of the <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-32p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-32P.svg">R10-32P</a> plaque.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Traffic Signal Signs And Plaques</div>


<span id="2b59" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.59 Traffic Signal Signs and Plaques (R10-5 through R10-30)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.59 &middot; manual page 215 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.59/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> To supplement traffic signal control, traffic signal (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-5.svg">R10-5</a> through <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-30/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-30.svg">R10-30</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be used to regulate road users.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> Traffic signal signs may be installed at certain locations to clarify signal control. Among the legends that may be used for this purpose are: A.LEFT (RIGHT) ON GREEN ARROW ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-5/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-5.svg">R10-5</a>), B.STOP HERE ON RED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-6.svg">R10-6</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-6a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-6a.svg">R10-6a</a>) for observance of stop lines, C.DO NOT BLOCK INTERSECTION (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-7.svg">R10-7</a>) for avoidance of traffic obstructions, D.USE LANE(S) WITH GREEN ARROW (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-8/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-8.svg">R10-8</a>) for obedience to lane-use control signals (see Chapter 4T), E.LEFT (RIGHT) TURN SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-10/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-10__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R10-10</a>), F.U TURN SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-10a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-10a.svg">R10-10a</a>) for exclusive control of a U-turn movement, G.U TURN YIELD TO RIGHT TURN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-16.svg">R10-16</a>), H.LEFT (RIGHT) TURN YIELD ON GREEN (symbolic circular green) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-12.svg">R10-12</a>), I.LEFT (RIGHT) TURN YIELD ON FLASHING YELLOW ARROW (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-12a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-12a.svg">R10-12a</a>), and J.LEFT (RIGHT) TURN YIELD ON FLASHING RED ARROW AFTER STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-27/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-27.svg">R10-27</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-28"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0274.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0274.webp" alt="Figure 2B-28. Traffic Signal Signs and Plaques"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-28. Traffic Signal Signs and Plaques <span class="src-page">p 274</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">02a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 22526 for the DO NOT BLOCK INTERSECTION (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-7/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-7.svg">R10-7</a>) sign.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02b</span> <span class="ca">The LEFT (RIGHT) TURN YIELD ON FLASHING RED ARROW AFTER STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-27/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-27.svg">R10-27</a>) is deleted. Refer to CVC 21457 for the  rules governing driver behavior at a flashing red indication at traffic signals.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> If used, the LEFT ON GREEN ARROW ONLY sign, the LEFT TURN SIGNAL sign, the LEFT TURN YIELD ON GREEN (symbolic circular green)) sign, the LEFT TURN YIELD ON FLASHING YELLOW ARROW sign, or the LEFT TURN YIELD ON FLASHING RED ARROW AFTER STOP) sign should be located adjacent to the left-turn signal face.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for errors in Paragraph 3 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If used, the RIGHT ON GREEN ARROW ONLY sign, the RIGHT TURN SIGNAL sign, the RIGHT TURN YIELD ON FLASHING YELLOW ARROW sign, or the RIGHT TURN YIELD ON FLASHING RED ARROW AFTER STOP sign should be located adjacent to the right-turn signal face.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> A U TURN YIELD TO RIGHT TURN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-16.svg">R10-16</a>) sign should be installed near the left-turn signal face if U-turns are allowed on a protected left-turn movement on an approach from which a right-turn GREEN ARROW signal indication is simultaneously being displayed to drivers making a right turn from the conflicting approach to their left. <span class="ca">5a  The U-TURN YIELD TO RIGHT TURN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-16/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-16.svg">R10-16</a>) sign is deleted as this condition should not be practiced. The actual movement  conflict should be eliminated rather than try to correct it with this sign. </span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> If used, a U TURN SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-10a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-10a.svg">R10-10a</a>) sign may be installed adjacent to the signal face that exclusively controls a U-turn movement.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> If needed for additional emphasis, an additional LEFT TURN YIELD ON GREEN (symbolic circular green) (R10- 12) sign with an AT SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-31p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-31P.svg">R10-31P</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be installed in advance of the intersection.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">07a</span> <span class="ca">If used, the location of this additional <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-12/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-12.svg">R10-12</a> sign should be in the raised median at the beginning of the left-turn lane, or be  based upon <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>, or as per engineering judgment.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07b</span> <span class="ca">The LEFT TURN ON GREEN ARROW ONLY – NO U TURN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sr39bca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sr39bca.webp">SR39B(CA)</a>) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-28ca">Figure 2B-28(CA)</a>) may be used at  signalized intersections with separate left turn phases to inform traffic that left turns can only be made on a green arrow in  accordance with CVC 21454 and “U” turns are prohibited.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">07b</span> <span class="ca">The LEFT OR U TURN ON GREEN ARROW ONLY (SR39A(U)(CA)) sign (refer to <a href="#fig-2b-28ca">Figure 2B-28(CA)</a>) may be used at signalized  intersections with separate left turn phases to inform traffic that left turns and “U” turns can only be made on a green arrow in  accordance with CVC 21454.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> In situations where traffic control signals are coordinated for progressive timing, the Traffic Signal Speed (I1-1) sign may be used (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2h/#2h04">Section 2H.04</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> The CROSSWALK—STOP ON RED (symbolic circular red) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-23/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-23.svg">R10-23</a>) and <span class="ca">or</span>STOP ON STEADY RED- YIELD ON FLASHING RED AFTER STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-23a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-23a.svg">R10-23a</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) shall only be used in conjunction with pedestrian hybrid beacons (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4j/#4j02">Section 4J.02</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">09a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to FHWA’s List of Known Errors for errors in Paragraph 9 text. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a04">Section 1A.04</a> for more details.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">10</span> The EMERGENCY SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-13/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-13.svg">R10-13</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) shall be used in conjunction with emergency- vehicle traffic control signals (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4m/#4m02">Section 4M.02</a>).</p>
<p><span class="pn">11</span> The EMERGENCY SIGNAL—STOP ON FLASHING RED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-14/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-14.svg">R10-14</a> or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-14a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-14a.svg">R10-14a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) shall be used in conjunction with emergency-vehicle hybrid beacons (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/4n/#4n02">Section 4N.02</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">12</span> If needed for extra emphasis, a STOP HERE ON FLASHING RED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-14b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-14b.svg">R10-14b</a>) sign may be installed with an emergency-vehicle hybrid beacon.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">13</span> The Left Turn Yield to Bicycles (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-12b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-12b.svg">R10-12b</a>) sign shall be limited to applications where the conflicting bicyclist movement would be unexpected in direction, location, or similar condition that would tend to violate the expectation of a turning motorist.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">14</span> The Left Turn Yield to Bicycles sign should be located adjacent to the left-turn signal face.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">15</span> If needed for additional emphasis, an additional Left Turn Yield to Bicycles sign with an AT SIGNAL (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-31p/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-31P.svg">R10-31P</a>) supplemental plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be installed in advance of the intersection for motor vehicles.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">15a</span> <span class="ca">If used, the location of this additional <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-12b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-12b.svg">R10-12b</a> sign should be in the raised median at the beginning of the left-turn lane, or be  based upon <a href="#fig-2c-3">Table 2C-3</a>, or as per engineering judgment.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">16</span> Where conditions might warrant additional emphasis to drivers turning at a signalized intersection where potential pedestrian conflicts might not be readily apparent, a Turning Vehicles Yield to (Stop for) Pedestrians (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-15.svg">R10-15</a>, R10- 15a) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be used.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">17</span> The Turning Vehicles Stop for Pedestrians (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-15a.svg">R10-15a</a>) sign shall only be used in jurisdictions where laws, ordinances or resolutions specifically require that a driver must stop for a pedestrian.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">17a</span> <span class="ca">The Turning Vehicles Stop for Pedestrians (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-15a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-15a.svg">R10-15a</a>) is deleted as a stop is not required in California per CVC 21950.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">18</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-15/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-15.svg">R10-15</a> series signs, where used, should be placed as follows: A.On the near right corner of the signalized intersection for right-turning vehicles. B.On the far left corner of the signalized intersection for the left-turning vehicles onto a two-way street. C.On the near left corner of the signalized intersection for left-turning vehicles from a one-way street onto a one- way street.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b60" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.60 No Turn on Red Signs (R10-11 Series, R10-17a, and R10-30)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.60 &middot; manual page 216 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.60/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> Where a right turn on a circular red signal indication (or a left turn on a circular red signal indication from a one-way street to a one-way street) is to be prohibited, a NO TURN ON RED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11.svg">R10-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11b.svg">R10-11b</a>) word message sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) shall be used. A NO TURN ON RED (symbolic circular red) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11a.svg">R10-11a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) shall be used when the approach is controlled by both circular red and red arrow indications.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">01a</span> <span class="ca">Refer to CVC 22101 for the No Turn on Red signs.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> If used, the No Turn on Red sign should be installed near the appropriate signal head.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">02a</span> <span class="ca">No Right Turn on Red sign may be used on the near right of skewed intersections where the adjacent approach leg to the left  intersects the road user’s approach leg at an angle of less than 75 degrees.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">02b</span> <span class="ca">A No Turn on Red sign or may be used on the near right of extremely wide intersections.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02c</span> <span class="ca">When used, the No Turn on Red sign should be placed where it will most easily be seen by the road user intending to turn. At  least one should be placed overhead, or at a right/left-hand corner facing approaching traffic.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> A No Turn on Red sign should be considered when an engineering study finds that one or more of the following conditions exists: A.Inadequate sight distance to vehicles approaching from the left (or right, if applicable); B.Geometrics or operational characteristics of the intersection that might result in unexpected conflicts; C.An exclusive pedestrian or bicycle phase; D.An unacceptable number of conflicting pedestrian movements with right-turn-on-red maneuvers, especially involving children, older pedestrians, or persons with disabilities; E.More than three right-turn-on-red crashes reported in a 12-month period for the particular approach; or F.The skew angle of the intersecting roadways creates difficulty for drivers to see traffic approaching from their left (or right, if applicable).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> If an <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11.svg">R10-11</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11a.svg">R10-11a</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11b/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11b.svg">R10-11b</a>, or <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-17a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-17a.svg">R10-17a</a> sign with conventional road size as shown in <a href="#fig-2b-1">Table 2B-1</a> is used on an approach on the far side of the intersection and the distance between the stop line and the sign is greater than 120 feet, then a duplicate sign shall be located on the near side of the intersection to supplement the sign on the far side of the intersection.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> When a no-turn-on-red restriction applies during certain time periods only, the following alternatives may be used: A.Movement Prohibition (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-1.svg">R3-1</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-2.svg">R3-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-4.svg">R3-4</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-18/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-18.svg">R3-18</a>, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r3-27/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R3-27.svg">R3-27</a>) signs or NO TURN ON RED signs displayed by using a<span class="ca">n</span><span class="ca">Activated </span>blank-out sign for the time period or one or more portion(s) of a particular cycle of the traffic control signal during which the prohibition is applicable; or B.Static signs incorporating a supplemental legend or with a supplemental <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-20ap/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-20aP__Sheet_1_of_2.svg">R10-20aP</a> plaque (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) showing the hours and days during which the prohibition is applicable.</p>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> White LEDs may be used in the border and activated during periods of turn prohibition to enhance the sign conspicuity.</p>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> On signalized approaches with more than one right-turn lane, a NO TURN ON RED EXCEPT FROM RIGHT LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11c/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11c.svg">R10-11c</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be post-mounted at the intersection or a NO TURN ON RED FROM THIS LANE (with down arrow) (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-11d/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-11d.svg">R10-11d</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be mounted over the approximate center of the lane from which turns on red are prohibited.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">08</span> Where turns on red are permitted and the signal indication is a steady RED ARROW, the RIGHT (LEFT) ON RED ARROW AFTER STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-17a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-17a.svg">R10-17a</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) should be installed adjacent to the RED ARROW signal indication.<span class="ca"> A circular red signal face should be used, instead of correcting the condition with this sign.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">08a</span> <span class="ca">The RIGHT (LEFT) ON RED ARROW AFTER STOP (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-17a/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-17a.svg">R10-17a</a>) sign is deleted as it compromises the meaning of the right red  arrow.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">09</span> A RIGHT TURN ON RED MUST YIELD TO U-TURN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-30/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-30.svg">R10-30</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-28">Figure 2B-28</a>) may be installed to remind road users that they must yield to conflicting U-turn traffic on the street or highway onto which they are turning right on a red signal after stopping.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b61" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.61 Ramp Metering Signs (R10-28 and R10-29)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.61 &middot; manual page 218 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.61/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">00a</span> <span class="ca">For State highways, refer to Caltrans’ Ramp Metering Design Manual. Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/1a/#1a05">Section 1A.05</a> for information regarding this  publication.</span></p>
<p><span class="pn">00b</span> <span class="ca">Refer to <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/2g/#2g102ca">Section 2G.102(CA)</a> for regulatory signs for HOV lanes at metered ramps.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> When ramp control signals (see Chapter 4P) are used to meter traffic on a freeway or expressway entrance ramp, regulatory signs with legends appropriate to the control may be installed adjacent to the ramp control signal faces.</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> For entrance ramps with only one controlled lane, an XX VEHICLE(S) PER GREEN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-28/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-28.svg">R10-28</a>) <span class="ca"><a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r89aca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r89aca.webp">R89A(CA)</a></span> sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-29">Figure 2B-29</a><span class="ca">(CA)</span>) may be used to inform road users of the number of vehicles that are permitted to proceed during each short display of the green signal indication. For entrance ramps with more than one controlled lane, an XX VEHICLE(S) PER GREEN EACH LANE (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-29/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-29.svg">R10-29</a>) (see <a href="#fig-2b-29">Figure 2B-29</a>) sign may be used to inform road users of the number of vehicles that are permitted to proceed from each lane during each short display of the green signal indication.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-29"></span><span id="fig-2b-29ca"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0276.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0276.webp" alt="Figure 2B-29. Ramp Metering Signs R10-28; Figure 2B-29(CA). California Ramp Metering Signs R89A(CA) 1 VEHICLE PER GREEN"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-29. Ramp Metering Signs R10-28; Figure 2B-29(CA). California Ramp Metering Signs R89A(CA) 1 VEHICLE PER GREEN <span class="src-page">p 276</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> Chapter 2L contains provisions for the use of blank-out or changeable message signs when the metering is limited by time, day, or condition.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> The STOP HERE ON RED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-6.svg">R10-6</a>) sign should be placed on Type 1 standards near the limit line at metered entrance ramps  with three or more lanes.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r10-6/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R10-6.svg">R10-6</a> sign is used to emphasize the required observance of the signal limit line, such as the metering signal controlling  traffic on metered freeway entrance ramps.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">06</span> The ALL VEHICLES STOP ON RED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r90-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r90-1ca.webp">R90-1(CA)</a>) sign should be placed when converting a non-metered HOV preferential lane  to a metered one.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">07</span> The <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r90-1ca/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/r90-1ca.webp">R90-1(CA)</a> sign may also be used on new installations where potential for confusion exists.</p>
</div>

<div class="subch">Road Closed And Weight Limit Signs</div>


<span id="2b62" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.62 KEEP OFF MEDIAN Sign (R11-1)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.62 &middot; manual page 219 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.62/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The KEEP OFF MEDIAN (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r11-1/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R11-1.svg">R11-1</a>) sign (see <a href="#fig-2b-30">Figure 2B-30</a>) may be used to prohibit driving into or parking on the median.</p>
</div>
<div class="mfig-inline single"><figure class="mfig"><span id="fig-2b-30"></span><span id="fig-2b-30ca"></span><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0277.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/ca-mutcd/p0277.webp" alt="Figure 2B-30. Road Closed and Weight Limit Signs R11-1 R11-2 R11-3 R11-3b R11-4 R12-1 R12-4 R12-2 R12-5 R12-7 R12-7aP R12-6 R11-3a; Figure 2B-30(CA). California Road Closed and Weight Limit Signs R36(CA) COMMERCIAL VEHICLES OVER TONS PROHIBITED 5 5 SR40(CA) OVER 13’- 6” WIDE"></a><figcaption>Figure 2B-30. Road Closed and Weight Limit Signs R11-1 R11-2 R11-3 R11-3b R11-4 R12-1 R12-4 R12-2 R12-5 R12-7 R12-7aP R12-6 R11-3a; Figure 2B-30(CA). California Road Closed and Weight Limit Signs R36(CA) COMMERCIAL VEHICLES OVER TONS PROHIBITED 5 5 SR40(CA) OVER 13’- 6” WIDE <span class="src-page">p 277</span></figcaption></figure></div>
<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> The KEEP OFF MEDIAN sign should be installed on the left-hand side of the roadway within the median at random intervals as needed wherever there is a tendency for encroachment.</p>
</div>

<span id="2b63" class="anchor"></span>

## 2B.63 ROAD CLOSED Sign (R11-2) and LOCAL TRAFFIC ONLY Signs (R11-3 Series, R11-4)

<div class="secmeta">Section 2B.63 &middot; manual page 219 &middot; <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/s/ca/2B.63/">short link</a></div>

<div class="prov gui"><div class="prov-label">Guidance</div>
<p><span class="pn">01</span> The ROAD CLOSED (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R11-2.svg">R11-2</a>) sign should be installed where roads have been closed to all traffic (except authorized vehicles).</p>
<p><span class="pn">02</span> ROAD CLOSED—LOCAL TRAFFIC ONLY (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r11-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R11-3.svg">R11-3</a>) or ROAD CLOSED TO THRU TRAFFIC (R11- 4) signs should be used where through traffic is not permitted, or for a closure some distance beyond the sign, but where the highway is open for local traffic up to the point of closure.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov std"><div class="prov-label">Standard</div>
<p><span class="pn">03</span> The Road Closed (<a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r11-2/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R11-2.svg">R11-2</a>, <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r11-3/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R11-3.svg">R11-3</a> series, and <a class="sref" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/r11-4/" data-svg="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/R11-4.svg">R11-4</a>) signs (see <a href="#fig-2b-30">Figure 2B-30</a>) shall be designed as horizontal rectangles. These signs shall be preceded by the applicable Advance Road Closed warning sign with the secondary <span class="ca">distance</span> legend <span class="ca">or</span> AHEAD and, if applicable, an Advance Detour warning sign (see <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/6h/#6h04">Section 6H.04</a>).</p>
</div>
<div class="prov opt"><div class="prov-label">Option</div>
<p><span class="pn">04</span> An intersecting street name or a well-known destination may be substituted for the XX MILES AHEAD legend in urban areas.</p>
<p><span class="pn">05</span> The word message BRIDGE OUT <span class="ca">or CLOSED</span> may be substituted for the ROAD CLOSED legend where applicable.</p>
</div>
<div class="prov sup"><div class="prov-label">Support</div>
